### Match Servers by Partial Name

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/server/class-use/RegisteredServer

Finds all `RegisteredServer`s whose names start with a given partial name.

```APIDOC
## GET /api/servers/match/{partialName}

### Description
Matches all `RegisteredServer`s whose names start with the provided partial name.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
`/api/servers/match/{partialName}`

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **partialName** (String) - Required - The partial name to match against server names.

#### Query Parameters
None

#### Request Body
None

### Request Example
None

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **servers** (Collection<RegisteredServer>) - A collection of registered servers matching the partial name.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "servers": [
    {
      "name": "server1_lobby",
      "address": "192.168.1.100:25565"
    },
    {
      "name": "server1_survival",
      "address": "192.168.1.101:25565"
    }
  ]
}
```
```

### Example: Sending Plugin Message to Server (Java)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/Player

Demonstrates how to send a plugin message to the server the player is connected to. It uses Optional chaining to safely access the current server and then sends the message.

```java
final ChannelIdentifier identifier;
final Player player;
player.getCurrentServer()
      .map(ServerConnection::getServer)
      .ifPresent((RegisteredServer server) -> {
        server.sendPluginMessage(identifier, data);
      });

```

--------------------------------

### Get Previous Server

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/server/class-use/RegisteredServer

Retrieves the server a player was connected to before their current connection.

```APIDOC
## GET /api/connections/{connectionId}/previous-server

### Description
Returns the server that the player associated with this connection was connected to before switching to this connection.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
`/api/connections/{connectionId}/previous-server`

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **connectionId** (String) - Required - The unique identifier for the player's connection.

#### Query Parameters
None

#### Request Body
None

### Request Example
None

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **previousServer** (Optional<RegisteredServer>) - The previously connected server, if available.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "previousServer": {
    "name": "old_server",
    "address": "192.168.1.50:25565"
  }
}
```
```json
{
  "previousServer": null
}
```
```

### Get Server Info from Registered Server

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/server/class-use/ServerInfo

Retrieve the ServerInfo associated with a registered server.

```APIDOC
## GET ServerInfo from Registered Server

### Description
Returns the `ServerInfo` for this server.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
`/servers/{serverName}/info`

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **serverName** (string) - Required - The name of the registered server.

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **ServerInfo** (object) - The server information object.
  - **name** (string) - The name of the server.
  - **address** (InetSocketAddress) - The network address of the server.
  - **groups** (Set<String>) - A set of group names the server belongs to.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "name": "survival_1",
  "address": {
    "host": "192.168.1.105",
    "port": 25568
  },
  "groups": ["survival", "world"]
}
```
```

### PermissionsSetupEvent Methods

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/permission/PermissionsSetupEvent

```APIDOC
## PermissionsSetupEvent Methods

### Description
Provides methods to retrieve event details and manipulate permission providers.

### Method
`getSubject()`
`getProvider()`
`createFunction(PermissionSubject subject)`
`setProvider(PermissionProvider provider)`
`toString()`

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
None

#### Query Parameters
None

#### Request Body
None

### Request Example
```json
{
  "method": "getSubject"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **subject** (PermissionSubject) - The subject whose permissions are being set up.
- **provider** (PermissionProvider) - The provider for permissions.
- **permissionFunction** (PermissionFunction) - A function to get permission details.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "subject": "Player_Instance",
  "provider": "DefaultPermissionProvider",
  "permissionFunction": "PermissionFunction_Instance"
}
```
```

### LoginEvent API

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/connection/LoginEvent

Details about the LoginEvent, including its purpose, constructor, and methods for getting and setting the event result.

```APIDOC
## LoginEvent

### Description
This event is fired once the player has been authenticated, but before they connect to a server. Velocity will wait for this event to finish firing before proceeding with the rest of the login process, but you should try to limit the work done in any event that fires during the login process.

### Method
N/A (Event Class)

### Endpoint
N/A (Event Class)

### Parameters
#### Constructor Parameters
- **player** (Player) - Required - The player object associated with the login attempt.

### Request Example
N/A (Event Class)

### Response
#### Success Response (Event Outcome)
- **getResult()** (ResultedEvent.ComponentResult) - Returns the current result of the login event.
- **setResult(ResultedEvent.ComponentResult result)** (void) - Sets the outcome of the login event. The provided result must be non-null.

#### Response Example
```java
// Example of getting the result
ResultedEvent.ComponentResult currentResult = loginEvent.getResult();

// Example of setting the result to deny connection
loginEvent.setResult(ResultedEvent.ComponentResult.denied(Component.text("Login denied.")));
```
```

--------------------------------

### Example: Using PluginMessageEncoder (Java)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/Player

Illustrates the usage of PluginMessageEncoder to send structured data as a plugin message. The lambda function defines how to write data to the output stream.

```java
final ChannelMessageSink target;
final ChannelIdentifier identifier;
final boolean result = target.sendPluginMessage(identifier, (output) -> {
  output.writeUTF("some input");
  output.writeInt(1);
});

```

### PluginDependency Constructor and Methods

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/plugin/meta/PluginDependency

Details on how to create and use PluginDependency objects, including methods to get plugin ID, version, and optional status.

```APIDOC
## PluginDependency Class

### Description
Represents a dependency on another plugin.

### Constructor
#### PluginDependency(String id, @Nullable String version, boolean optional)

Creates a new instance of PluginDependency.

**Parameters:**
- **id** (String) - Required - The plugin ID.
- **version** (String) - Optional - The version the dependency should match.
- **optional** (boolean) - Required - Whether the dependency is optional.

### Methods
#### getId()

Returns the plugin ID of this `PluginDependency`.

**Returns:**
- (String) - The plugin ID.

#### getVersion()

Returns the version this `PluginDependency` should match.

**Returns:**
- (Optional<String>) - An `Optional` with the plugin version, which may be empty.

#### isOptional()

Returns whether the dependency is optional for the plugin to work correctly.

**Returns:**
- (boolean) - true if the dependency is optional, false otherwise.

#### equals(Object o)

Overrides the `equals` method from the `Object` class.

#### hashCode()

Overrides the `hashCode` method from the `Object` class.

#### toString()

Overrides the `toString` method from the `Object` class.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "id": "placeholder_plugin",
  "version": "1.0.0",
  "optional": false
}
```

### Response Example
```json
{
  "pluginId": "placeholder_plugin",
  "version": "1.0.0",
  "optional": false
}
```
```

### Get Login Rate Limit

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/config/ProxyConfig

Retrieves the configured rate limit for player logins. This prevents players from logging in too rapidly, which can help mitigate brute-force attacks.

```APIDOC
## GET /proxy/loginRatelimit

### Description
Get the limit for how long a player must wait to log back in.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
/proxy/loginRatelimit

### Parameters
#### Query Parameters
None

### Request Example
None

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **loginRatelimit** (integer) - The login rate limit in milliseconds.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "loginRatelimit": 5000
}
```
```

--------------------------------

### ListenerBoundEvent Details

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/proxy/ListenerBoundEvent

Details about the ListenerBoundEvent, which is fired by the proxy after a listener starts accepting connections.

```APIDOC
## ListenerBoundEvent

### Description
This event is fired by the proxy after a listener starts accepting connections.

### Method
N/A (Event Class)

### Endpoint
N/A (Event Class)

### Parameters
#### Constructor Parameters
- **address** (InetSocketAddress) - The network address the listener is bound to.
- **listenerType** (ListenerType) - The type of listener that was bound.

### Request Example
N/A

### Response
#### Event Data
- **address** (InetSocketAddress) - The network address the listener is bound to.
- **listenerType** (ListenerType) - The type of listener that was bound.

#### Response Example
N/A
```

### Get Plugin Description - Java

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/plugin/PluginContainer

Retrieves the description of the plugin loaded by the proxy. This method returns a PluginDescription object containing metadata about the plugin.

```java
PluginDescription getDescription()
Returns the plugin's description.
```

--------------------------------

### Get All Registered Servers (Java)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/index-all

Retrieves all `RegisteredServer` instances that are currently registered with the Velocity proxy. This allows for server management and routing.

```java
import com.velocitypowered.api.proxy.ProxyServer;
import com.velocitypowered.api.proxy.server.RegisteredServer;
import java.util.Collection;

// Assuming 'proxy' is an instance of ProxyServer
Collection<RegisteredServer> allServers = proxy.getAllServers();
System.out.println("Registered servers: " + allServers.size());
```

--------------------------------

### PlayerChatEvent Result Manipulation

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/player/class-use/PlayerChatEvent

This section covers how to get and set the chat result for a player event.

```APIDOC
## PlayerChatEvent

### Description
Provides methods to interact with and modify the result of a player chat event.

### Methods

#### `PlayerChatEvent.ChatResult getResult()`

##### Description
Retrieves the current result of the player chat event.

##### Method
Getter

##### Endpoint
N/A (Instance method)

#### `void setResult(PlayerChatEvent.ChatResult result)`

##### Description
Sets the result for the player chat event. Deprecated: for 1.19.1 and newer, setting this as denied will kick users.

##### Method
Setter

##### Endpoint
N/A (Instance method)

### Parameters

#### Path Parameters
None

#### Query Parameters
None

#### Request Body
None

### Request Example
```javascript
// Example of setting the result to denied
playerChatEvent.setResult(PlayerChatEvent.ChatResult.denied());

// Example of getting the current result
const currentResult = playerChatEvent.getResult();
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **void** - No return value for `setResult`.
- **PlayerChatEvent.ChatResult** (Object) - The result object for `getResult`.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "example": "N/A for void method, or PlayerChatEvent.ChatResult object"
}
```
```

### PlayerEnterConfigurationEvent Constructor and Methods (Java)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/player/configuration/PlayerEnterConfigurationEvent

This Java code snippet demonstrates the constructor and key methods of the PlayerEnterConfigurationEvent record class. It includes methods for accessing player and server information, as well as standard object methods like toString, hashCode, and equals.

```java
public record PlayerEnterConfigurationEvent(@NotNull Player player, ServerConnection server) extends Record {

    public PlayerEnterConfigurationEvent(@NotNull Player player, ServerConnection server) {
        this.player = player;
        this.server = server;
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return "PlayerEnterConfigurationEvent[player=" + player + ", server=" + server + "]";
    }

    @Override
    public int hashCode() {
        return Objects.hash(player, server);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean equals(Object o) {
        if (this == o) return true;
        if (o == null || getClass() != o.getClass()) return false;
        PlayerEnterConfigurationEvent that = (PlayerEnterConfigurationEvent) o;
        return Objects.equals(player, that.player) && Objects.equals(server, that.server);
    }

    @NotNull
    public Player player() {
        return player;
    }

    public ServerConnection server() {
        return server;
    }
}
```

### KeySigned Interface Methods (Java)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/crypto/KeySigned

This Java interface defines methods for handling signatures of signed objects. It includes functionalities to get the signature, the public key of the signer, expiry details, and salt. It also provides methods to check for expiration and validate the signature.

```Java
public interface KeySigned {
    Instant getExpiryTemporal();
    default byte[] getSalt() {
        return null;
    }
    @org.checkerframework.checker.nullness.qual.Nullable byte[] getSignature();
    PublicKey getSigner();
    default boolean hasExpired() {
        return Instant.now().isAfter(getExpiryTemporal());
    }
    @Beta default boolean isSignatureValid() {
        // Implementation details omitted for brevity
        return false; 
    }
}
```

### ProxyServer Interface Methods

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/ProxyServer

Provides an interface to a Minecraft server proxy, with methods to manage servers, players, and proxy settings.

```APIDOC
## ProxyServer Interface Methods

### Description
Provides an interface to a Minecraft server proxy, with methods to manage servers, players, and proxy settings.

### Methods

#### Server Management

- **`closeListeners()`** (void): Closes all listening endpoints for this server.
- **`createRawRegisteredServer(ServerInfo server)`** (`RegisteredServer`): Creates a raw `RegisteredServer` without tying it into the internal server map.
- **`registerServer(ServerInfo server)`** (`RegisteredServer`): Registers a server with this proxy.
- **`unregisterServer(ServerInfo server)`**: Unregisters this server from the proxy.
- **`getServer(String name)`** (`Optional<RegisteredServer>`): Retrieves a registered `RegisteredServer` instance by its name.
- **`getAllServers()`** (`Collection<RegisteredServer>`): Retrieves all `RegisteredServer`s registered with this proxy.
- **`matchServer(String partialName)`** (`Collection<RegisteredServer>`): Matches all `RegisteredServer`s whose names start with the provided partial name.

#### Player Management

- **`getAllPlayers()`** (`Collection<Player>`): Retrieves all players currently connected to this proxy.
- **`getPlayer(String username)`** (`Optional<Player>`): Retrieves the player currently connected to this proxy by their Minecraft username.
- **`getPlayer(UUID uuid)`** (`Optional<Player>`): Retrieves the player currently connected to this proxy by their Minecraft UUID.
- **`getPlayerCount()`** (`int`): Returns the number of players currently connected to this proxy.
- **`matchPlayer(String partialName)`** (`Collection<Player>`): Matches all `Player`s whose names start with the provided partial name.

#### Configuration and Information

- **`getBoundAddress()`** (`InetSocketAddress`): Gets the address that this proxy is bound to.
- **`getChannelRegistrar()`** (`ChannelRegistrar`): Gets the `ChannelRegistrar` instance.
- **`getCommandManager()`** (`CommandManager`): Gets the `CommandManager` instance.
- **`getConfiguration()`** (`ProxyConfig`): Gets the `ProxyConfig` instance.
- **`getConsoleCommandSource()`** (`ConsoleCommandSource`): Returns an instance of `CommandSource` that can be used to determine if the command is being invoked by the console or a console-like executor.
- **`getEventManager()`** (`EventManager`): Gets the `EventManager` instance.
- **`getPluginManager()`** (`PluginManager`): Gets the `PluginManager` instance.
- **`getScheduler()`** (`Scheduler`): Gets the `Scheduler` instance.
- **`getVersion()`** (`ProxyVersion`): Returns the version of the proxy.
- **`isShuttingDown()`** (`boolean`): Returns whether the proxy is currently shutting down.

#### Resource Pack Management

- **`createResourcePackBuilder(String url)`** (`ResourcePackInfo.Builder`): Creates a builder to build a `ResourcePackInfo` instance for use with `Player.sendResourcePackOffer(ResourcePackInfo)`.

#### Proxy Lifecycle

- **`shutdown()`**: Shuts down the proxy, kicking players with the default reason.
- **`shutdown(Component reason)`**: Shuts down the proxy, kicking players with the specified `reason`.
```

--------------------------------

### KeySigned Interface Methods

This section details the methods available for the KeySigned interface, allowing you to interact with signed object signatures.

```APIDOC
## KeySigned Interface Methods

This section details the methods available for the KeySigned interface, allowing you to interact with signed object signatures.

### `getExpiryTemporal()`

**Description**: Returns the expiry time point of the key. Note: this limit is arbitrary. RSA keys don't expire, but the signature of this key as provided by the session server will expire.

**Method**: N/A (Interface Method)

**Endpoint**: N/A

### `getSalt()`

**Description**: Returns the signature salt or null if not salted.

**Method**: N/A (Interface Method)

**Endpoint**: N/A

### `getSignature()`

**Description**: Retrieves the signature of the signed object.

**Method**: N/A (Interface Method)

**Endpoint**: N/A

### `getSigner()`

**Description**: Returns the key used to sign the object.

**Method**: N/A (Interface Method)

**Endpoint**: N/A

### `hasExpired()`

**Description**: Check if the signature has expired.

**Method**: N/A (Interface Method)

**Endpoint**: N/A

### `isSignatureValid()`

**Description**: Validates the signature, expiry temporal and key against the signer public key. Note: This will **not** check for expiry. You can check for expiry with `hasExpired()`.
DOES NOT WORK YET FOR MESSAGES AND COMMANDS!
Addendum: Does not work for 1.19.1 until the user has authenticated.

**Method**: N/A (Interface Method)

**Endpoint**: N/A

### Get Minecraft Channel Identifier Components

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/messages/MinecraftChannelIdentifier

Illustrates how to extract the namespace, name, and full ID from a MinecraftChannelIdentifier object. These methods are used to inspect an identifier's properties after it has been created or retrieved.

```java
import com.velocitypowered.api.proxy.messages.MinecraftChannelIdentifier;

MinecraftChannelIdentifier identifier = MinecraftChannelIdentifier.create("myplugin", "channel");

String namespace = identifier.getNamespace(); // "myplugin"
String name = identifier.getName();           // "channel"
String id = identifier.getId();             // "myplugin:channel"

// You can also convert it back to a Key
// Key key = identifier.asKey();
```

### QueryResponse Methods

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/server/QueryResponse

This section details the various methods available on the QueryResponse object to retrieve information about the server.

```APIDOC
## GET /query/response

### Description
Retrieves information from a GS4 query response.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
`/query/response`

### Parameters
#### Query Parameters
This endpoint does not have query parameters.

#### Request Body
This endpoint does not have a request body.

### Request Example
N/A

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **hostname** (String) - The hostname of the server.
- **gameVersion** (String) - The game version of the server.
- **map** (String) - The current map name.
- **currentPlayers** (int) - The current number of online players.
- **maxPlayers** (int) - The maximum number of players allowed.
- **proxyHost** (String) - The public-facing hostname of the proxy.
- **proxyPort** (int) - The public-facing port of the proxy.
- **players** (Collection<String>) - A collection of currently online player names.
- **proxyVersion** (String) - The name and version of the server software.
- **plugins** (Collection<PluginInformation>) - A collection of plugin information.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "hostname": "MyVelocityServer",
  "gameVersion": "1.19.4",
  "map": "world",
  "currentPlayers": 50,
  "maxPlayers": 100,
  "proxyHost": "play.example.com",
  "proxyPort": 25565,
  "players": [
    "Player1",
    "Player2"
  ],
  "proxyVersion": "Velocity 3.1.2",
  "plugins": [
    {
      "name": "ExamplePlugin",
      "version": "1.0.0"
    }
  ]
}
```

### Methods

## Instance Methods

### getHostname
#### Description
Get hostname which will be used to reply to the query. By default it is `ProxyConfig.getMotd()` in plain text without colour codes.

#### Returns
`String` - hostname

### getGameVersion
#### Description
Get game version which will be used to reply to the query. By default supported Minecraft versions range is sent.

#### Returns
`String` - game version

### getMap
#### Description
Get map name which will be used to reply to the query. By default `ProxyConfig.getQueryMap()` is sent.

#### Returns
`String` - map name

### getCurrentPlayers
#### Description
Get current online player count which will be used to reply to the query.

#### Returns
`int` - online player count

### getMaxPlayers
#### Description
Get max player count which will be used to reply to the query.

#### Returns
`int` - max player count

### getProxyHost
#### Description
Get proxy (public facing) hostname.

#### Returns
`String` - proxy hostname

### getProxyPort
#### Description
Get proxy (public facing) port.

#### Returns
`int` - proxy port

### getPlayers
#### Description
Get collection of players which will be used to reply to the query.

#### Returns
`Collection<String>` - collection of players

### getProxyVersion
#### Description
Get server software (name and version) which will be used to reply to the query.

#### Returns
`String` - server software

### getPlugins
#### Description
Get list of plugins which will be used to reply to the query.

#### Returns
`Collection<QueryResponse.PluginInformation>` - collection of plugins

### toBuilder
#### Description
Creates a new `QueryResponse.Builder` instance from data represented by this response, so that you may create a new `QueryResponse` with new data. It is guaranteed that `queryResponse.toBuilder().build().equals(queryResponse)`: that is, if no other changes are made to the returned builder, the built instance will equal the original instance.

#### Returns
`QueryResponse.Builder` - `QueryResponse` builder

## Static Methods

### builder
#### Description
Creates a new `QueryResponse.Builder` instance.

#### Returns
`QueryResponse.Builder` - `QueryResponse` builder

## Inherited Methods

### equals
#### Description
Overrides `equals` in class `Object`.

### hashCode
#### Description
Overrides `hashCode` in class `Object`.

### toString
#### Description
Overrides `toString` in class `Object`.

```

### Proxy Configuration Settings

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/config/ProxyConfig

This section details the various configuration settings available through the ProxyConfig interface. These settings control aspects of the proxy's behavior, including network connections, command handling, and player management.

```APIDOC
## GET /proxy/config

### Description
Retrieves various proxy configuration settings.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
/proxy/config

### Parameters
None

### Request Example
None

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **attemptConnectionOrder** (List<String>) - The order of servers players will be connected to.
- **commandRatelimit** (int) - The rate limit for command execution.
- **compressionLevel** (int) - The compression level for packets.
- **compressionThreshold** (int) - The minimum compression threshold for packets.
- **connectTimeout** (int) - The timeout duration for establishing connections.
- **favicon** (Optional<Favicon>) - The proxy favicon shown in the tab list.
- **forcedHosts** (Map<String,List<String>>) - Forced servers mapped to a given virtual host.
- **kickAfterRateLimitedCommands** (int) - The kick limit for rate-limited commands.
- **kickAfterRateLimitedTabCompletes** (int) - The kick limit for rate-limited tab completions.
- **loginRatelimit** (int) - The limit for how long a player must wait to log back in.
- **motd** (Component) - The MOTD component shown in the tab list.
- **queryMap** (String) - The map name reported to GameSpy 4 query services.
- **queryPort** (int) - The port GameSpy 4 queries are accepted on.
- **readTimeout** (int) - The duration before a read timeout occurs.
- **servers** (Map<String,String>) - A map of all servers registered in `velocity.toml`.
- **showMaxPlayers** (int) - The maximum players shown in the tab list.
- **tabCompleteRatelimit** (int) - The rate limit for tab completions.
- **announceForge** (boolean) - Whether the proxy displays support for Forge/FML.
- **forwardCommandsIfRateLimited** (boolean) - Whether to forward commands to the backend if the player is rate-limited.
- **kickOnCommandRateLimit** (boolean) - Whether to kick players who are command rate-limited.
- **kickOnTabCompleteRateLimit** (boolean) - Whether to kick players who are tab complete rate-limited.
- **onlineMode** (boolean) - Whether the proxy is in online mode.
- **queryEnabled** (boolean) - Whether GameSpy 4 queries are accepted by the proxy.
- **preventClientProxyConnections** (boolean) - Whether to prevent client proxy connections by checking the IP with Mojang servers.
- **queryShowPlugins** (boolean) - Whether GameSpy 4 queries should show plugins installed on Velocity.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "attemptConnectionOrder": [
    "survival",
    "creative"
  ],
  "commandRatelimit": 100,
  "compressionLevel": 6,
  "compressionThreshold": 256,
  "connectTimeout": 5000,
  "favicon": "மையானjavapackage.com/favicon.png",
  "forcedHosts": {
    "example.com": ["survival", "creative"]
  },
  "kickAfterRateLimitedCommands": 5,
  "kickAfterRateLimitedTabCompletes": 3,
  "loginRatelimit": 60000,
  "motd": {
    "text": "Welcome to Velocity!",
    "color": "aqua"
  },
  "queryMap": "MyServerMap",
  "queryPort": 25565,
  "readTimeout": 30000,
  "servers": {
    "survival": "192.168.1.10:25565",
    "creative": "192.168.1.11:25566"
  },
  "showMaxPlayers": 100,
  "tabCompleteRatelimit": 20,
  "announceForge": true,
  "forwardCommandsIfRateLimited": false,
  "kickOnCommandRateLimit": true,
  "kickOnTabCompleteRateLimit": true,
  "onlineMode": true,
  "queryEnabled": true,
  "preventClientProxyConnections": true,
  "queryShowPlugins": true
}
```
```

### CommandSource in com.velocitypowered.api.command

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/command/class-use/CommandSource

This section outlines methods and constructors related to CommandSource within the com.velocitypowered.api.command package, focusing on command execution and source retrieval.

```APIDOC
## CommandSource in com.velocitypowered.api.command

### Description
This section details how `CommandSource` is used within the command framework of the Velocity API. It includes methods that return a `CommandSource`, methods that accept `CommandSource` as a parameter, and constructors that utilize `CommandSource`.

### Methods Returning CommandSource
*   **`CommandInvocation.source()`**: Returns the source that executed the command.

### Methods With CommandSource Parameters
*   **`CommandManager.executeAsync(CommandSource source, String cmdLine)`**: Attempts to asynchronously execute a command.
*   **`CommandManager.executeImmediatelyAsync(CommandSource source, String cmdLine)`**: Attempts to execute a command asynchronously without firing a `CommandExecuteEvent`.
*   **`CommandManager.hasCommand(String alias, CommandSource source)`**: Checks if a command alias is registered and usable by the `CommandSource`.
*   **`CommandManager.offerBrigadierSuggestions(CommandSource source, String cmdLine)`**: Asynchronously collects suggestions for a command line.
*   **`CommandManager.offerSuggestions(CommandSource source, String cmdLine)`**: Asynchronously collects suggestions for a command line.

### Methods Returning Types With CommandSource Arguments
*   **`CommandMeta.getHints()`**: Returns command nodes that provide argument metadata and tab-complete suggestions.
*   **`BrigadierCommand.getNode()`**: Returns the literal node for this command.
*   **`BrigadierCommand.literalArgumentBuilder(String name)`**: Creates a `LiteralArgumentBuilder`.
*   **`BrigadierCommand.requiredArgumentBuilder(String name, com.mojang.brigadier.arguments.ArgumentType<T> argumentType)`**: Creates a `RequiredArgumentBuilder`.

### Method Parameters With CommandSource Type Arguments
*   **`CommandMeta.Builder.hint(com.mojang.brigadier.tree.CommandNode<CommandSource> node)`**: Specifies a command node for hints and suggestions.

### Constructor Parameters With CommandSource Type Arguments
*   **`BrigadierCommand(com.mojang.brigadier.builder.LiteralArgumentBuilder<CommandSource> builder)`**: Constructs a `BrigadierCommand` from a builder.
*   **`BrigadierCommand(com.mojang.brigadier.tree.LiteralCommandNode<CommandSource> node)`**: Constructs a `BrigadierCommand` from a command node.
```

--------------------------------

### Player Management API

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/ProxyServer

Endpoints for retrieving and matching player information on the proxy.

```APIDOC
## GET /api/players/{username}

### Description
Retrieves the player currently connected to this proxy by their Minecraft username. The search is case-insensitive.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
`/api/players/{username}`

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **username** (string) - Required - The username of the player to retrieve.

#### Response

- **server** (string) - The name of the server the player is currently connected to.

  "server": "survival"

### RegisteredServer API

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/server/RegisteredServer

This section details the methods available for interacting with a RegisteredServer.

```APIDOC
## RegisteredServer Interface

### Description
Represents a server that has been registered with the proxy. The `Audience` associated with a `RegisteredServer` represent all players on the server connected to this proxy and do not interact with the server in any way.

### Methods

#### `getServerInfo()`

##### Description
Returns the `ServerInfo` for this server.

##### Method
GET

##### Endpoint
N/A (Interface Method)

##### Returns
- `ServerInfo` - the server info

#### `getPlayersConnected()`

##### Description
Returns a list of all the players currently connected to this server on this proxy.

##### Method
GET

##### Endpoint
N/A (Interface Method)

##### Returns
- `Collection<Player>` - the players on this proxy

#### `ping()`

##### Description
Attempts to ping the remote server and return the server list ping result.

##### Method
GET

##### Endpoint
N/A (Interface Method)

##### Returns
- `CompletableFuture<ServerPing>` - the server ping result from the server

#### `ping(PingOptions pingOptions)`

##### Description
Attempts to ping the remote server and return the server list ping result according to the options provided.

##### Method
GET

##### Endpoint
N/A (Interface Method)

##### Parameters

*   **pingOptions** (PingOptions) - Required - the options provided for pinging the server

##### Returns
- `CompletableFuture<ServerPing>` - the server ping result from the server

##### Since
3.2.0
```

### TabCompleteEvent API

Details about the TabCompleteEvent, including its constructor and methods.

```APIDOC
## TabCompleteEvent

### Description
This event is fired after a tab complete response is sent by the remote server, for clients on 1.12.2 and below. You have the opportunity to modify the response sent to the remote player. Velocity will wait for this event to finish firing before sending the tab complete results to the client. Be sure to be as fast as possible, since the client will freeze while it waits for the tab complete results.

### Method
Event Listener

### Endpoint
N/A (Event)

### Parameters
#### Constructor Parameters
- **player** (Player) - Required - The player requesting the tab completion.
- **partialMessage** (String) - Required - The message being partially completed.
- **suggestions** (List<String>) - Required - The initial list of suggestions.

### Request Example
N/A

### Response
#### Event Modifications
- **getSuggestions()** (List<String>) - Returns a mutable list of suggestions that can be modified.

#### Response Example
N/A

### PlayerChooseInitialServerEvent

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/player/PlayerChooseInitialServerEvent

Fired when a player has finished the login process, and Velocity needs to choose the first server to connect to. This event allows modification of the initial server selection.

```APIDOC
## PlayerChooseInitialServerEvent

### Description
Fired when a player has finished the login process, and we need to choose the first server to connect to. Velocity will wait on this event to finish firing before initiating the connection but you should try to limit the work done in this event. Failures will be handled by `KickedFromServerEvent` as normal.

### Method
Event

### Endpoint
N/A (Event)

### Parameters
#### Constructor Parameters
- **player** (Player) - The player that was connected.
- **initialServer** (RegisteredServer | null) - The initial server selected, may be null.

### Methods
- **getPlayer()**: Returns the `Player` associated with this event.
- **getInitialServer()**: Returns an `Optional` containing the `RegisteredServer` that was initially selected.
- **setInitialServer(RegisteredServer server | null)**: Sets the new initial server for the player. Accepts `null` to indicate no server should be chosen.
- **toString()**: Returns a string representation of the event.
```

### ProxyQueryEvent Details

Provides details about the ProxyQueryEvent, including its purpose, constructors, and methods for handling proxy queries.

```APIDOC
## ProxyQueryEvent

### Description
This event is fired if the proxy is being queried over the GS4 Query protocol. Velocity will wait for this event to fire before providing a response to the client.

### Nested Classes
* `ProxyQueryEvent.QueryType` - Represents the type of query the client is asking for.

### Constructor Summary
* `ProxyQueryEvent(ProxyQueryEvent.QueryType queryType, InetAddress querierAddress, QueryResponse response)` - Creates a new event.

### Method Summary
* `getQuerierAddress()` - Get the address of the client that sent this query.
* `getQueryType()` - Returns the kind of query the remote client is performing.
* `getResponse()` - Returns the current query response.
* `setResponse(QueryResponse response)` - Sets a new query response.
* `toString()` - Overrides the toString method from Object.

### Constructor Details
#### ProxyQueryEvent
```java
public ProxyQueryEvent(ProxyQueryEvent.QueryType queryType, InetAddress querierAddress, QueryResponse response)
```
Creates a new event.

**Parameters:**
* `queryType` (ProxyQueryEvent.QueryType) - the type of query
* `querierAddress` (InetAddress) - the remote address for the query
* `response` (QueryResponse) - the current query response

### Method Details
#### getQueryType
```java
public ProxyQueryEvent.QueryType getQueryType()
```
Returns the kind of query the remote client is performing.

**Returns:**
* `ProxyQueryEvent.QueryType` - query type

#### getQuerierAddress
```java
public InetAddress getQuerierAddress()
```
Get the address of the client that sent this query.

**Returns:**
* `InetAddress` - querier address

#### getResponse
```java
public QueryResponse getResponse()
```
Returns the current query response.

**Returns:**
* `QueryResponse` - the current query response

### Resource Pack Information

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/player/ResourcePackInfo

This section details the methods available for accessing information about a resource pack.

```APIDOC
## GET /websites/jd_papermc_io_velocity_3_4_0/resourcepackinfo

### Description
Retrieves information about a specific resource pack.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
/websites/jd_papermc_io_velocity_3_4_0/resourcepackinfo

### Parameters
#### Query Parameters
- **id** (UUID) - Required - The unique identifier of the resource pack.
- **url** (String) - Required - The URL where the resource pack can be found.
- **prompt** (Component) - Optional - The message displayed to the user when the resource pack is applied.
- **force** (boolean) - Optional - A flag indicating whether the resource pack acceptance is enforced.
- **hash** (byte[]) - Optional - The SHA-1 hash of the resource pack file.
- **origin** (ResourcePackInfo.Origin) - Optional - The origin of the resource pack.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "id": "a1b2c3d4-e5f6-7890-1234-567890abcdef",
  "url": "http://example.com/resourcepacks/pack.zip",
  "prompt": {
    "text": "Please accept this resource pack."
  },
  "force": true,
  "hash": "a1b2c3d4e5f678901234567890abcdef12345678",
  "origin": "PACK"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **id** (UUID) - The unique identifier of the resource pack.
- **url** (String) - The URL where the resource pack can be found.
- **prompt** (Component) - The message displayed to the user when the resource pack is applied (null if not set).
- **force** (boolean) - True if the resource pack acceptance is enforced, false otherwise.
- **hash** (byte[]) - The SHA-1 hash of the resource pack file (null if not set).
- **origin** (ResourcePackInfo.Origin) - The origin of the resource pack.
- **originalOrigin** (ResourcePackInfo.Origin) - The original origin of the resource pack before any potential alterations.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "id": "a1b2c3d4-e5f6-7890-1234-567890abcdef",
  "url": "http://example.com/resourcepacks/pack.zip",
  "prompt": {
    "text": "Please accept this resource pack."
  },
  "force": true,
  "hash": "a1b2c3d4e5f678901234567890abcdef12345678",
  "origin": "PACK",
  "originalOrigin": "SERVER"
}
```

### Methods inherited from interface net.kyori.adventure.resource.ResourcePackRequestLike
- **asResourcePackRequest** (ResourcePackRequest) - Converts this resource pack information into a `ResourcePackRequest` object.
```

### PlayerFinishedConfigurationEvent Constructor (Java)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/player/configuration/PlayerFinishedConfigurationEvent

Shows the constructor for creating an instance of PlayerFinishedConfigurationEvent. It takes the Player and ServerConnection as parameters to initialize the event object.

```java
public PlayerFinishedConfigurationEvent(@NotNull Player player, @NotNull ServerConnection server)
```

--------------------------------

### Plugin Management API

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/plugin/PluginManager

Provides methods to manage plugins, including retrieving, checking, and adding plugins to the classpath.

```APIDOC
## GET /plugin/fromInstance

### Description
Gets the plugin container from an instance.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
/plugin/fromInstance

### Parameters
#### Query Parameters
- **instance** (Object) - Required - The instance to retrieve the plugin container for.

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **container** (PluginContainer) - The plugin container associated with the instance.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "container": {}
}
```

## GET /plugin/getPlugin

### Description
Retrieves a `PluginContainer` based on its ID.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
/plugin/getPlugin

### Parameters
#### Query Parameters
- **id** (String) - Required - The ID of the plugin to retrieve.

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **plugin** (PluginContainer) - The `PluginContainer` if available.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "plugin": {}
}
```

## GET /plugin/getPlugins

### Description
Gets a `Collection` of all `PluginContainer`s.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
/plugin/getPlugins

### Response
#### Success Response (200)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/command/RawCommand

Details about the RawCommand interface and its nested classes.

```APIDOC
## Interface RawCommand

### Description
A specialized sub-interface of `Command` which indicates the proxy should pass the command and its arguments directly without further processing. This is useful for bolting on external command frameworks to Velocity.

### Superinterfaces
- `Command`
- `InvocableCommand<RawCommand.Invocation>`

### Nested Classes
#### `static interface RawCommand.Invocation`
##### Description
Contains the invocation data for a raw command.

### Methods Inherited from `InvocableCommand`
- `execute`
- `hasPermission`
- `suggest`
- `suggestAsync`
```

### PostCommandInvocationEvent API

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/command/PostCommandInvocationEvent

Details about the PostCommandInvocationEvent, including its constructors and methods for retrieving command source, command string, and execution result.

```APIDOC
## PostCommandInvocationEvent

### Description
This event is fired when Velocity has executed a command. This event is called after the command execution has been handled. Commands can be cancelled or forwarded to backend servers in `CommandExecuteEvent`, which will prevent firing this event.

### Method
Event

### Endpoint
N/A (Event)

### Parameters
N/A

### Request Example
N/A

### Response
#### Event Data
- **commandSource** (`CommandSource`) - The source of the command execution.
- **command** (`String`) - The command string executed, without the leading slash.
- **result** (`CommandResult`) - The result of the command execution.

#### Response Example
N/A (Event Data)

## Constructor Summary

### PostCommandInvocationEvent
`PostCommandInvocationEvent(@NotNull CommandSource commandSource, @NotNull String command, @NotNull CommandResult result)`
Constructs a PostCommandInvocationEvent.

## Method Summary

### getCommandSource
`@NotNull CommandSource getCommandSource()`
Gets the source of this executed command.

### getCommand
`@NotNull String getCommand()`
Gets the original command line executed without the first slash.

### getResult
`@NotNull CommandResult getResult()`
Returns the result of the command execution.

### toString
`String toString()`
Overrides the default toString method.
```

### ServerConnectedEvent

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/player/ServerConnectedEvent

Details about the ServerConnectedEvent, including its constructor and methods.

```APIDOC
## ServerConnectedEvent

### Description
This event is fired once the player has successfully connected to the target server and the connection to the previous server has been de-established.

**Note**: For historical reasons, Velocity does wait on this event to finish firing before continuing the server connection process. This behavior is **deprecated** and likely to be removed in Polymer.

### Constructor
#### `ServerConnectedEvent(Player player, RegisteredServer server, @Nullable RegisteredServer previousServer)`
Constructs a ServerConnectedEvent.

**Parameters**:
- `player` (Player) - The player that was connected.
- `server` (RegisteredServer) - The server the player was connected to.
- `previousServer` (@Nullable RegisteredServer) - The server the player was previously connected to, null if none.

### Methods
#### `getPlayer()`
Returns the player associated with this event.

**Returns**:
- `Player`

#### `getServer()`
Returns the server the player connected to.

**Returns**:
- `RegisteredServer`

#### `getPreviousServer()`
Returns the server the player was previously connected to.

**Returns**:
- `Optional<RegisteredServer>`

#### `toString()`
Overrides the toString method from Object.

**Returns**:
- `String`
```

### GameProfileRequestEvent

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/player/GameProfileRequestEvent

Represents an event fired after PreLoginEvent to set up a player's game profile, allowing for custom configurations like skin replacement.

```APIDOC
## GameProfileRequestEvent

### Description
This event is fired after the `PreLoginEvent` in order to set up the game profile for the user. This can be used to configure a custom profile for a user, i.e. skin replacement. Velocity will wait for this event to finish firing before proceeding with the rest of the login process, but you should try to limit the work done in any event that fires during the login process.

### Method
Event Handling

### Endpoint
N/A (Event)

### Parameters
#### Constructor Parameters
- **connection** (InboundConnection) - Required - The connection connecting to the proxy.
- **originalProfile** (GameProfile) - Required - The original `GameProfile` for the user.
- **onlineMode** (boolean) - Required - Whether or not the user connected in online or offline mode.

#### Event Methods
- **getConnection()** - Returns the `InboundConnection` associated with this event.
- **getGameProfile()** - Returns the `GameProfile` that will be used to initialize the connection with. If no profile is currently specified, the one generated by the proxy (for offline mode) or retrieved from the Mojang session servers (for online mode) will be returned instead.
- **getOriginalProfile()** - Returns the original `GameProfile` of the user.
- **getUsername()** - Returns the username of the player.
- **isOnlineMode()** - Returns `true` if the user connected in online mode, `false` otherwise.
- **setGameProfile(GameProfile gameProfile)** - Sets the `GameProfile` to use for this connection. Setting to `null` uses the original profile.

### Request Example
```java
@Subscribe
public void onGameProfileRequest(GameProfileRequestEvent event) {
    // Example: Set a custom GameProfile (e.g., for skin replacement)
    GameProfile originalProfile = event.getOriginalProfile();
    GameProfile customProfile = new GameProfile(originalProfile.getId(), originalProfile.getName());
    // Add properties for custom skin, etc.
    event.setGameProfile(customProfile);
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response
No direct response object, but the `GameProfile` can be modified via `setGameProfile()`.

#### Response Example
N/A
```

--------------------------------

### Command Execution and Messaging

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/command/CommandSource

The CommandSource interface provides methods to send plain and rich messages. It extends Audience, PermissionSubject, and Pointered interfaces, inheriting their functionalities.

```APIDOC
## CommandSource Interface Documentation

### Description
The `CommandSource` interface represents anything that can execute a `Command`. It provides methods for sending messages to the source.

### Interfaces
- `Audience`
- `PermissionSubject`
- `Pointered`

### Methods

#### `sendPlainMessage`
```java
default void sendPlainMessage(@NotNull String message)
```
##### Description
Sends a plain message to this source. Legacy color codes are deprecated.

##### Parameters
- **message** (`String`) - The plain message content.

#### `sendRichMessage` (String)
```java
default void sendRichMessage(@NotNull String message)
```
##### Description
Sends a message with the MiniMessage format to this source.

##### Parameters
- **message** (`String`) - The MiniMessage content.

#### `sendRichMessage` (String, TagResolver...)
```java
default void sendRichMessage(@NotNull String message, @NotNull TagResolver... resolvers)
```
##### Description
Sends a message with the MiniMessage format and provided tag resolvers to this source.

##### Parameters
- **message** (`String`) - The MiniMessage content.
- **resolvers** (`TagResolver...`) - The tag resolvers to use for message formatting.

### Inherited Methods
This interface inherits methods from `Audience`, `PermissionSubject`, and `Pointered`, including functionalities for:
- Sending various types of messages (action bar, boss bar, title, player list)
- Sound playback
- Resource pack management
- Permission checking
- Pointer operations
```

### GameProfile.Property Methods

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/util/GameProfile

Provides methods to retrieve the name, value, and signature of a profile property.

```APIDOC
## GameProfile.Property Methods

### Description
Provides methods to retrieve the name, value, and signature of a profile property.

### Methods

#### `getName()`
- **Description**: Returns the name of the property.
- **Return Type**: `String`

#### `getValue()`
- **Description**: Returns the value of the property.
- **Return Type**: `String`

#### `getSignature()`
- **Description**: Returns the Mojang signature for the property.
- **Return Type**: `String`

#### `toString()`
- **Description**: Returns a string representation of the property.
- **Return Type**: `String`

### Request Example
```java
GameProfile.Property property = new GameProfile.Property("name", "value", "signature");
String name = property.getName();
String value = property.getValue();
String signature = property.getSignature();
String toString = property.toString();
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **`name`** (String) - The name of the property.
- **`value`** (String) - The value of the property.
- **`signature`** (String) - The Mojang signature for the property.
- **`toString`** (String) - String representation of the property.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "name": "exampleName",
  "value": "exampleValue",
  "signature": "exampleSignature"
}
```
```

### PermissionFunction API Documentation

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/permission/PermissionFunction

This section details the PermissionFunction interface, including its fields and methods for managing permission values.

```APIDOC
## PermissionFunction Interface

### Description
A functional interface that calculates permission settings for a given `PermissionSubject`. It can be used as the assignment target for a lambda expression or method reference.

### Fields

*   **ALWAYS_TRUE** (`PermissionFunction`) - A pre-defined function that always returns `Tristate.TRUE`.
*   **ALWAYS_FALSE** (`PermissionFunction`) - A pre-defined function that always returns `Tristate.FALSE`.
*   **ALWAYS_UNDEFINED** (`PermissionFunction`) - A pre-defined function that always returns `Tristate.UNDEFINED`.

### Methods

#### getPermissionValue

*   **Description**: Gets the subject's setting for a particular permission.
*   **Method**: `Tristate getPermissionValue(String permission)`
*   **Parameters**:
    *   `permission` (String) - The permission to check.
*   **Returns**: (Tristate) - The value the permission is set to.

```

### ServerLoginPluginMessageEvent Details

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/player/ServerLoginPluginMessageEvent

This section details the ServerLoginPluginMessageEvent, including its constructors, methods, and nested classes.

```APIDOC
## ServerLoginPluginMessageEvent

Fired when a server sends a login plugin message to the proxy. Plugins have the opportunity to respond to the messages as needed. Velocity will wait on this event to finish. The server will be responsible for continuing the login process once the server is satisfied with any login plugin responses sent by proxy plugins (or messages indicating a lack of response).

### Nested Class Summary

`static class ServerLoginPluginMessageEvent.ResponseResult` - The result class, containing a response to the login plugin message sent by the server.

### Constructor Summary

`ServerLoginPluginMessageEvent(ServerConnection connection, ChannelIdentifier identifier, byte[] contents, int sequenceId)` - Constructs a new `ServerLoginPluginMessageEvent`.

### Method Summary

- `contentsAsDataStream()` - Returns the contents of the login plugin message sent by the server as an `ByteArrayDataInput`.
- `contentsAsInputStream()` - Returns the contents of the login plugin message sent by the server as an `InputStream`.
- `getConnection()` - Returns the connection on which the plugin message was sent.
- `getContents()` - Returns a copy of the contents of the login plugin message sent by the server.
- `getIdentifier()` - Returns the channel identifier for the message sent.
- `getResult()` - Returns the result associated with this event.
- `getSequenceId()` - Returns the ID of the message.
- `setResult(ServerLoginPluginMessageEvent.ResponseResult result)` - Sets the result of this event. The result must be non-null.
- `toString()` - Overrides the default `toString` method from `Object`.

### Constructor Details

#### ServerLoginPluginMessageEvent
```java
public ServerLoginPluginMessageEvent(ServerConnection connection, ChannelIdentifier identifier, byte[] contents, int sequenceId)
```
**Parameters:**
- `connection` (ServerConnection) - the connection on which the plugin message was sent
- `identifier` (ChannelIdentifier) - the channel identifier for the message sent
- `contents` (byte[]) - the contents of the message
- `sequenceId` (int) - the ID of the message

### Method Details

#### getConnection
```java
public ServerConnection getConnection()
```

#### getIdentifier
```java
public ChannelIdentifier getIdentifier()
```

#### getContents
```java
public byte[] getContents()
```

**Returns:**
- (byte[]) the contents of the message

#### contentsAsInputStream

```
Returns the contents of the login plugin message sent by the server as an `InputStream`.

**Returns:**
- (ByteArrayInputStream) the contents of the message as a stream

#### contentsAsDataStream

public ByteArrayDataInput contentsAsDataStream()
```

**Returns:**

#### getResult

```

## Player Management

### PlayerFinishConfigurationEvent

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/player/configuration/PlayerFinishConfigurationEvent

This event is executed when a player is about to finish the configuration state. Velocity waits for this event before asking the client to finish the configuration state.

```APIDOC
## PlayerFinishConfigurationEvent

### Description
This event is executed when a player is about to finish the configuration state. Velocity will wait for this event before asking the client to finish the configuration state. However due to backend server being unable to keep the connection alive during state changes, Velocity will only wait for a maximum of 5 seconds. If you need to hold a player in configuration state, use the `PlayerConfigurationEvent`.

Since: 3.3.0
Since Minecraft: 1.20.2

### Method
This is an event, not an HTTP endpoint.

### Parameters
This event is triggered automatically and does not take direct API parameters.

#### Record Components
- **player** (`@NotNull Player`) - The player who is about to finish the configuration phase.
- **server** (`@NotNull ServerConnection`) - The server that has (re-)configured the player.

### Request Example
This is an event, no request body is applicable.

### Response
#### Event Trigger
This event is triggered by the Velocity server.

#### Response Example
N/A
```

--------------------------------

### Endpoint

### Parameters

  - **id** (string) - A unique ID for the resource pack.
  - **size** (integer) - The size of the resource pack in bytes.
  - **hash** (string) - The SHA-1 hash of the resource pack.
  - **required** (boolean) - Whether the resource pack is required.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "packInfo": {
    "url": "http://example.com/resourcepack.zip",
    "id": "pack1",
    "size": 10485760,
    "hash": "abcdef1234567890abcdef1234567890abcdef12",
    "required": false
  }
}
```

### Response

No content.

#### Response Example

### PluginContainer API

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/plugin/PluginContainer

This section details the methods available through the PluginContainer interface for interacting with loaded plugins.

```APIDOC
## PluginContainer API

### Description

Provides access to methods for retrieving information and services associated with a loaded plugin.

### Methods

#### `getDescription()`

**Description:** Returns the plugin's description.

**Returns:**
- `PluginDescription`: The plugin's description.

#### `getInstance()`

**Description:** Returns the created plugin instance if it is available.

**Returns:**
- `Optional<?>`: An Optional containing the plugin instance if available, otherwise an empty Optional.

#### `getExecutorService()`

**Description:** Returns an executor service for this plugin. The executor will use a cached thread pool.

**Returns:**
- `ExecutorService`: An `ExecutorService` associated with this plugin.
```

```APIDOC
## Class BrigadierCommand

### Description
A command that uses Brigadier for parsing the command and providing suggestions to the client.

### Constructors

- **BrigadierCommand**(`@NotNull LiteralArgumentBuilder<CommandSource> builder`): Constructs a `BrigadierCommand` from the node returned by the given builder.
- **BrigadierCommand**(`@NotNull LiteralCommandNode<CommandSource> node`): Constructs a `BrigadierCommand` from the given command node.

### Methods

- **getNode**(): Returns the literal node for this command.
- **literalArgumentBuilder**(`@NotNull String name`): Creates a new LiteralArgumentBuilder of the required name.
- **requiredArgumentBuilder**(`@NotNull String name`, `@NotNull ArgumentType<T> argumentType`): Creates a new RequiredArgumentBuilder of the required name and type.

### Fields

- **FORWARD** (`static final int`): The return code used by a `Command` to indicate the command execution should be forwarded to the backend server.

{
  "message": "Example request body not applicable for class documentation."
}
```

### Response Example

{
  "message": "Example response body not applicable for class documentation."
}
```

### LoginPhaseConnection API

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/LoginPhaseConnection

Provides methods for managing connections during the login phase, including sending and consuming plugin messages.

```APIDOC
## LoginPhaseConnection

### Description
Represents a connection that is in the login phase. This is most useful in conjunction with login plugin messages.

### Nested Classes
#### LoginPhaseConnection.MessageConsumer
Consumes the message.

### Methods
#### sendLoginPluginMessage
Sends a login plugin message to the client, and provides a consumer to react to the response to the client. The login process will not continue until there are no more login plugin messages that require responses.

- **Method**: void
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (Interface Method)

#### Parameters
- **identifier** (ChannelIdentifier) - Required - The channel identifier to use.
- **contents** (byte[]) - Required - The message to send.
- **consumer** (LoginPhaseConnection.MessageConsumer) - Required - The consumer that will respond to the message.

### Inherited Methods
- **From `InboundConnection`**: `getHandshakeIntent`, `getProtocolState`, `getProtocolVersion`, `getRawVirtualHost`, `getRemoteAddress`, `getVirtualHost`, `isActive`
- **From `KeyIdentifiable`**: `getIdentifiedKey`
```

### Create Favicon from Image or Path (Java)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/util/class-use/Favicon

Provides static methods for creating Favicon objects. The `create(BufferedImage image)` method generates a Favicon from an in-memory image, while `create(Path path)` creates one by loading an image from a specified file path.

```java
Favicon faviconFromImage = Favicon.create(bufferedImage);
```

```java
Favicon faviconFromPath = Favicon.create(imagePath);
```

### TabList Management API

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/player/TabList

This section covers the core methods for managing the player tab list, including adding, removing, and clearing entries, and setting or clearing the header and footer.

```APIDOC
## TabList Management

This API provides methods to manipulate the player's tab list.

### addEntry

Adds a `TabListEntry` to the `Player`'s tab list.

**Method:** void

**Endpoint:** N/A (Method within an interface)

**Parameters:**
* **entry** (`TabListEntry`) - Required - The entry to add to the tab list.

**Throws:**
* `IllegalArgumentException` - on versions below 1.19.3, if an entry with the same UUID already exists

### addEntries (Iterable)

Adds a `Iterable` of `TabListEntry`'s to the `Player`'s tab list.

**Method:** default void

**Endpoint:** N/A (Method within an interface)

**Parameters:**
* **entries** (`Iterable<TabListEntry>`) - Required - An iterable collection of entries to add to the tab list.

**Throws:**
* `IllegalArgumentException` - on versions below 1.19.3, if an entry with the same UUID already exists

### addEntries (Array)

Adds an array of `TabListEntry`'s to the `Player`'s tab list.

**Method:** default void

**Endpoint:** N/A (Method within an interface)

**Parameters:**
* **entries** (`TabListEntry...`) - Required - An array of entries to add to the tab list.

**Throws:**
* `IllegalArgumentException` - on versions below 1.19.3, if an entry with the same UUID already exists

### removeEntry

Removes the `TabListEntry` from the tab list with the `GameProfile` identified with the specified `UUID`.

**Method:** Optional<TabListEntry>

**Endpoint:** N/A (Method within an interface)

**Parameters:**
* **uuid** (`UUID`) - Required - The UUID of the entry to remove.

**Returns:**
* `Optional<TabListEntry>` - An Optional containing the removed `TabListEntry` if present, otherwise `Optional.empty()`.

### containsEntry

Determines if the specified entry exists in the tab list.

**Method:** boolean

**Endpoint:** N/A (Method within an interface)

**Parameters:**
* **uuid** (`UUID`) - Required - The UUID of the entry to check.

**Returns:**
* `boolean` - `true` if the entry exists, `false` otherwise.

### getEntry

Retrieves the tab list entry associated with the given uuid.

**Method:** Optional<TabListEntry>

**Endpoint:** N/A (Method within an interface)

**Parameters:**
* **uuid** (`UUID`) - Required - The player's `UUID` the `TabListEntry` is in reference to.

**Returns:**
* `Optional<TabListEntry>` - An `Optional` containing the `TabListEntry` if found, otherwise `Optional.empty()`.

### getEntries

Returns an immutable `Collection` of the `TabListEntry`s in the tab list.

**Method:** Collection<TabListEntry>

**Endpoint:** N/A (Method within an interface)

**Returns:**
* `Collection<TabListEntry>` - An immutable collection of tab list entries.

### clearAll

Clears all entries from the tab list.

**Method:** void

**Endpoint:** N/A (Method within an interface)

### setHeaderAndFooter

Deprecated. Use `Audience.sendPlayerListHeaderAndFooter(Component, Component)` instead.
Sets the tab list header and footer for the player.

**Method:** @Deprecated void

**Endpoint:** N/A (Method within an interface)

**Parameters:**
* **header** (`Component`) - Required - The header component.
* **footer** (`Component`) - Required - The footer component.

### clearHeaderAndFooter

Clears the tab list header and footer for the player.

**Method:** void

**Endpoint:** N/A (Method within an interface)

```

### Server Management API

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/ProxyServer

Endpoints for managing registered servers within the Velocity proxy.

```APIDOC
## GET /api/servers/{name}

### Description
Retrieves a registered `RegisteredServer` instance by its name. The search is case-insensitive.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
`/api/servers/{name}`

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **name** (string) - Required - The name of the server to retrieve.

#### Response
##### Success Response (200)
- **serverName** (string) - The name of the registered server.
- **serverAddress** (string) - The network address of the server.

##### Response Example
```json
{
  "serverName": "lobby",
  "serverAddress": "localhost:25565"
}
```

## GET /api/servers

### Description
Retrieves all `RegisteredServer`s registered with this proxy.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
`/api/servers`

### Parameters
None

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **servers** (array) - A collection of registered servers.
  - **serverName** (string) - The name of the registered server.
  - **serverAddress** (string) - The network address of the server.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "servers": [
    {
      "serverName": "lobby",
      "serverAddress": "localhost:25565"
    },
    {
      "serverName": "survival",
      "serverAddress": "192.168.1.100:25566"
    }
  ]
}
```

## POST /api/servers/register

### Description
Registers a server with this proxy. A server with this name should not already exist.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
`/api/servers/register`

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **serverInfo** (object) - Required - Information about the server to register.
  - **name** (string) - Required - The name of the server.
  - **address** (string) - Required - The network address of the server (e.g., 'localhost:25565').

### Request Example
```json
{
  "serverInfo": {
    "name": "minigame",
    "address": "10.0.0.5:25567"
  }
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **message** (string) - Confirmation of successful registration.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "message": "Server 'minigame' registered successfully."
}
```

## DELETE /api/servers/unregister

### Description
Unregisters a server from the proxy.

### Method
DELETE

### Endpoint
`/api/servers/unregister`

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **serverInfo** (object) - Required - Information about the server to unregister.
  - **name** (string) - Required - The name of the server.
  - **address** (string) - Required - The network address of the server.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "serverInfo": {
    "name": "minigame",
    "address": "10.0.0.5:25567"
  }
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **message** (string) - Confirmation of successful unregistration.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "message": "Server 'minigame' unregistered successfully."
}
```

## GET /api/servers/match/{partialName}

### Description
Matches all `RegisteredServer`s whose names start with the provided partial name.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
`/api/servers/match/{partialName}`

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **partialName** (string) - Required - The partial name to match servers against.

#### Response
##### Success Response (200)
- **servers** (array) - A collection of matched registered servers.
  - **serverName** (string) - The name of the matched server.
  - **serverAddress** (string) - The network address of the matched server.

##### Response Example
```json
{
  "servers": [
    {
      "serverName": "survival_1",
      "serverAddress": "192.168.1.101:25566"
    },
    {
      "serverName": "survival_2",
      "serverAddress": "192.168.1.102:25566"
    }
  ]
}
```
```

### IdentifiedKey API

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/crypto/IdentifiedKey

This section details the methods for interacting with IdentifiedKey objects, which represent signed public keys.

```APIDOC
## IdentifiedKey Interface

### Description
Represents session-server cross-signed dated RSA public key.

### Methods

#### `getSignedPublicKey()`

**Description:** Returns the RSA public key. Note: this key is at least 2048 bits but may be larger.

**Method:** GET

**Endpoint:** N/A (Method within an object)

**Return Type:** `PublicKey`

**Return Description:** The RSA public key.

#### `verifyDataSignature(byte[] signature, byte[]... toVerify)`

**Description:** Validates a signature against this public key.

**Method:** POST (logical operation, not a typical HTTP POST)

**Endpoint:** N/A (Method within an object)

**Parameters:**

*   **Path Parameters:** None

*   **Query Parameters:** None

*   **Request Body:**
    *   `signature` (byte[]) - Required - The signature data.
    *   `toVerify` (byte[]...) - Required - The data to verify.

**Return Type:** `boolean`

**Return Description:** `true` if the signature is valid, `false` otherwise.

#### `getSignatureHolder()`

**Description:** Retrieves the signature holder's UUID. Returns null before the `LoginEvent`.

**Method:** GET

**Endpoint:** N/A (Method within an object)

**Return Type:** `UUID` (Nullable)

**Return Description:** The holder UUID or null if not present.

#### `getKeyRevision()`

**Description:** Retrieves the key revision.

**Method:** GET

**Endpoint:** N/A (Method within an object)

**Return Type:** `IdentifiedKey.Revision`

**Return Description:** The key revision.

### Nested Classes

#### `IdentifiedKey.Revision`

**Description:** The different versions of player keys, per Minecraft version.

```

### PingOptions Class

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/server/PingOptions

Provides immutable parameters for pinging a RegisteredServer. Includes options for protocol version, timeout, and virtual host.

```APIDOC
## PingOptions Class

### Description
Contains the parameters used to ping a `RegisteredServer`. This class is immutable.

### Fields
- **DEFAULT** (PingOptions) - Required - Default PingOptions.

### Methods
- **builder()** - static PingOptions.Builder - Creates a new builder for `PingOptions`.
- **getProtocolVersion()** - ProtocolVersion - Returns the protocol version used to ping the server.
- **getTimeout()** - long - Returns the maximum period of time to wait for a response from the remote server in milliseconds.
- **getVirtualHost()** - @Nullable String - Returns the virtual host to pass to the server for the ping.

### Nested Classes
- **PingOptions.Builder** - A builder for `PingOptions` objects.
```

--------------------------------

### CookieReceiveEvent Details

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/player/CookieReceiveEvent

Details about the CookieReceiveEvent, including its purpose, constructor, and methods for handling cookie responses.

```APIDOC
## CookieReceiveEvent

### Description
This event is fired when a cookie response from a client is received by the proxy. Velocity will wait on this event to finish firing before discarding the received cookie (if handled) or forwarding it to the backend server.

### Method
Event

### Endpoint
N/A (Event)

### Parameters
#### Constructor Parameters
- **player** (Player) - The player who sent the cookie response.
- **key** (Key) - The identifier of the cookie.
- **data** (byte[]) - The data of the cookie.

### Request Example
N/A (Event)

### Response
#### Success Response (Event Handling)
- **getResult()** (CookieReceiveEvent.ForwardResult) - Returns the result associated with this event.
- **setResult(CookieReceiveEvent.ForwardResult result)** - Sets the result of this event. The result must be non-null.

#### Response Example
```java
// Example of handling the event to prevent forwarding
EVENT_BUS.subscribe(CookieReceiveEvent.class, event -> {
    event.setResult(CookieReceiveEvent.ForwardResult.NOT_FORWARD);
});
```

### Methods
- **getPlayer()** (Player) - Returns the player associated with the event.
- **getOriginalKey()** (Key) - Returns the original key of the cookie.
- **getOriginalData()** (byte[]) - Returns the original data of the cookie.
- **getResult()** (CookieReceiveEvent.ForwardResult) - Returns the result of the event.
- **setResult(CookieReceiveEvent.ForwardResult result)** - Sets the result of the event.
- **toString()** (String) - Returns a string representation of the event.
```

### Proxy Control API

```APIDOC
## POST /api/proxy/shutdown

### Description
Shuts down the proxy, kicking players with the specified reason.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
`/api/proxy/shutdown`

### Parameters
#### Request Body

### Request Example

{
  "reason": "Server is restarting. Please join back in a few minutes."
}
```

### Response

- **message** (string) - Confirmation that the shutdown command has been issued.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "message": "Proxy shutdown initiated."
}
```

### Endpoint

```

## GET /api/proxy/configuration

### Description
Gets the `ProxyConfig` instance, which contains the proxy's configuration.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
`/api/proxy/configuration`

### Parameters
None

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **config** (object) - The proxy configuration details.
  - **tryDirectLogin** (boolean) - Whether direct login is attempted.
  - **queryEnabled** (boolean) - Whether the query protocol is enabled.
  - **logSync** (boolean) - Whether logging is synchronized.
  - **velocityProxiedNodes** (boolean) - Whether Velocity proxied nodes are used.
  - **playerInfoCacheSize** (integer) - The size of the player info cache.
  - **forceDefaultBrand** (boolean) - Whether to force the default brand.
  - **throttledOnlineMode** (boolean) - Whether online mode is throttled.
  - **ipForwarding** (boolean) - Whether IP forwarding is enabled.
  - **remoteConsoleEnabled** (boolean) - Whether remote console is enabled.
  - ** shutdownsEnabled ** (boolean) - Whether shutdowns are enabled.
  - **pluginMessages** (array) - List of allowed plugin messages.
  - **timeouts** (object) - Various timeout settings.
    - **login** (integer) - Login timeout in milliseconds.
    - **read** (integer) - Read timeout in milliseconds.
    - **write** (integer) - Write timeout in milliseconds.
  - **playerHealthChecks** (object) - Player health check settings.
    - **enabled** (boolean) - Whether player health checks are enabled.
    - **interval** (integer) - Interval for health checks in milliseconds.
    - **timeout** (integer) - Timeout for health checks in milliseconds.
  - **tryPlayerListUpdates** (boolean) - Whether to try player list updates.
  - **announcePlayerArrivals** (boolean) - Whether to announce player arrivals.
  - **announcePlayerDepartures** (boolean) - Whether to announce player departures.
  - **loginGracePeriod** (integer) - Grace period for login in milliseconds.
  - **authentication** (object) - Authentication settings.
    - **viaVersion** (boolean) - Whether ViaVersion is used for authentication.
    - **uuidSource** (string) - The source for UUIDs.
  - **advanced** (object) - Advanced configuration options.
    - **compressionLevel** (integer) - Compression level for network traffic.
    - **packetSnipping** (boolean) - Whether packet snipping is enabled.
    - **packetThreshold** (integer) - Packet threshold for optimizations.
  - **pingPassthrough** (boolean) - Whether ping passthrough is enabled.
  - **showUnknownServers** (boolean) - Whether to show unknown servers in the server list.
  - **forwardingMode** (string) - The mode for IP forwarding.
  - **legacyForgeSupport** (boolean) - Whether legacy Forge support is enabled.
  - **tabList** (object) - Tab list configuration.
    - **pingFormat** (string) - Format for player ping in tab list.
    - **enabled** (boolean) - Whether the tab list is enabled.
  - **bossBar** (object) - Boss bar configuration.
    - **enabled** (boolean) - Whether the boss bar is enabled.
    - **message** (string) - Message displayed in the boss bar.
  - **preventProxyChain** (boolean) - Whether to prevent proxy chain.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "config": {
    "tryDirectLogin": true,
    "queryEnabled": false,
    "logSync": false,
    "velocityProxiedNodes": false,
    "playerInfoCacheSize": 1000,
    "forceDefaultBrand": false,
    "throttledOnlineMode": false,
    "ipForwarding": true,
    "remoteConsoleEnabled": false,
    "shutdownsEnabled": true,
    "pluginMessages": [
      "minecraft:register",
      "minecraft:unregister"
    ],
    "timeouts": {
      "login": 5000,
      "read": 60000,
      "write": 60000
    },
    "playerHealthChecks": {
      "enabled": true,
      "interval": 5000,
      "timeout": 3000
    },
    "tryPlayerListUpdates": true,
    "announcePlayerArrivals": true,
    "announcePlayerDepartures": true,
    "loginGracePeriod": 5000,
    "authentication": {
      "viaVersion": false,
      "uuidSource": "mojang"
    },
    "advanced": {
      "compressionLevel": 6,
      "packetSnipping": false,
      "packetThreshold": 256
    },
    "pingPassthrough": true,
    "showUnknownServers": false,
    "forwardingMode": "modern",
    "legacyForgeSupport": false,
    "tabList": {
      "pingFormat": "${ping}ms",
      "enabled": true
    },
    "bossBar": {
      "enabled": false,
      "message": ""
    },
    "preventProxyChain": false
  }
}
```

### CommandSource in com.velocitypowered.api.event.command

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/command/class-use/CommandSource

This section details the role of CommandSource in command-related events within the Velocity API, including event creation and retrieval of the command's source.

```APIDOC
## CommandSource in com.velocitypowered.api.event.command

### Description
This section focuses on the usage of `CommandSource` within the event system for command executions in the Velocity API. It covers methods for retrieving the `CommandSource` from events and constructors for creating these events.

### Methods Returning CommandSource
*   **`CommandExecuteEvent.getCommandSource()`**: Gets the `CommandSource` responsible for executing the command.
*   **`PostCommandInvocationEvent.getCommandSource()`**: Retrieves the `CommandSource` of the executed command.

### Constructor Parameters With CommandSource Type Arguments
*   **`CommandExecuteEvent(CommandSource commandSource, String command)`**: Constructs a `CommandExecuteEvent`.
*   **`CommandExecuteEvent(CommandSource commandSource, String command, CommandExecuteEvent.InvocationInfo invocationInfo)`**: Constructs a `CommandExecuteEvent` with invocation information.
*   **`PostCommandInvocationEvent(CommandSource commandSource, String command, CommandResult result)`**: Constructs a `PostCommandInvocationEvent`.
```
