### Event Handling

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/index-all

APIs for registering event listeners with the Velocity proxy's event manager.

```APIDOC
## Event Handling

### Description
These methods allow plugins to register listeners for various events within the Velocity proxy. Both asynchronous and synchronous event listening are supported.

### Methods

#### `EventManager.register(Object, Class<E>, short, EventHandler<E>)`

- **Description**: Registers an event handler for a specific event type, associated with a plugin, and includes a priority.
- **Parameters**:
  - `plugin` (Object) - Required - The plugin instance registering the listener.
  - `eventClass` (Class<E>) - Required - The class of the event to listen for.
  - `priority` (short) - Required - The priority of the event handler.
  - `handler` (EventHandler<E>) - Required - The event handler implementation.

#### `EventManager.register(Object, Class<E>, EventHandler<E>)`

- **Description**: Registers an event handler for a specific event type, associated with a plugin, with a default priority.
- **Parameters**:
  - `plugin` (Object) - Required - The plugin instance registering the listener.
  - `eventClass` (Class<E>) - Required - The class of the event to listen for.
  - `handler` (EventHandler<E>) - Required - The event handler implementation.

#### `EventManager.register(Object, Class<E>, PostOrder, EventHandler<E>)`

- **Description**: Registers an event handler with a specified order of execution.
- **Deprecated**: Use `EventManager.register(Object, Class, short, EventHandler)` instead.

#### `EventManager.register(Object, Object)`

- **Description**: Registers a listener object to handle events associated with a plugin.
- **Parameters**:
  - `plugin` (Object) - Required - The plugin instance registering the listener.
  - `listener` (Object) - Required - The listener object containing event handling methods.
```

--------------------------------

### Server Ping Options

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/index-all

Allows setting protocol and virtual host for server ping options.

```APIDOC
## POST /api/servers/ping-options

### Description
Configures ping options for a server, including protocol version and virtual host.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/api/servers/ping-options

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **serverAddress** (string) - Required - The address of the server to ping.
- **protocolVersion** (integer) - Optional - The protocol version to use for the ping.
- **virtualHost** (string) - Optional - The virtual host to pass to the server during the ping.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "serverAddress": "mc.example.com",
  "protocolVersion": 756,
  "virtualHost": "proxy.example.com"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **message** (string) - A confirmation message indicating the options have been set.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "message": "Ping options updated for server."
}
```
```

Methods for interacting with player dialogs and simulating chat input, with a note on unimplemented dialog functionality.

Displays a dialog to the player. **This method is not currently implemented in Velocity and will not perform any actions.**

### Method

### Endpoint
N/A (Method within an interface)

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
None

#### Query Parameters
None

#### Request Body
None

### Request Example

// Example of calling showDialog (no actual effect currently)
player.showDialog(someDialogLikeObject);
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
N/A (Method performs no action)

#### Response Example
N/A

### Description
Sends chat input onto the player's current server as if they typed it into the client chat box.

### Method

### Endpoint
N/A (Method within an interface)

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
None

#### Query Parameters
None

#### Request Body
None

### Request Example

// Example of spoofing chat input

```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
N/A (Method sends input)

#### Response Example
N/A

### PlayerEnterConfigurationEvent

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/player/configuration/PlayerEnterConfigurationEvent

This event is executed when a player is about to enter the configuration state. It is not called for the initial configuration of a player after login. Velocity will wait for this event before asking the client to enter configuration state.

```APIDOC
## PlayerEnterConfigurationEvent

### Description
This event is executed when a player is about to enter the configuration state. It is **not** called for the initial configuration of a player after login. Velocity will wait for this event before asking the client to enter configuration state.

### Method
N/A (Event)

### Endpoint
N/A (Event)

### Parameters
#### Record Components
- **player** (Player) - Required - The player who is about to enter configuration state.
- **server** (ServerConnection) - Required - The server that wants to reconfigure the player.

### Request Example
N/A

### Response
#### Event Details
- **player** (Player) - The player associated with the event.
- **server** (ServerConnection) - The server associated with the event.

#### Response Example
N/A
```

### ProtocolVersion Enum Methods

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/network/ProtocolVersion

This section details the various methods available for the ProtocolVersion enum, allowing you to retrieve protocol information, check support status, and convert between different representations.

```APIDOC
## ProtocolVersion Enum Documentation

This document outlines the methods available for the `ProtocolVersion` enum.

### Methods

*   **`values()`**: Returns an array containing the constants of this enum class, in the order they are declared.
    *   **Returns**: An array of `ProtocolVersion` constants.

*   **`valueOf(String name)`**: Returns the enum constant of this class with the specified name.
    *   **Description**: The string must match *exactly* an identifier used to declare an enum constant in this class. Extraneous whitespace characters are not permitted.
    *   **Parameters**:
        *   `name` (String) - The name of the enum constant to be returned.
    *   **Returns**: The enum constant with the specified name.
    *   **Throws**:
        *   `IllegalArgumentException` - If this enum class has no constant with the specified name.
        *   `NullPointerException` - If the argument is null.

*   **`getProtocol()`**: Returns the protocol as an int.
    *   **Returns**: The protocol version as an integer.

*   **`getName()`**: Deprecated. Returns the user-friendly name for this protocol.
    *   **Description**: A protocol may be shared by multiple versions. Use `getVersionIntroducedIn()` or `getVersionsSupportedBy()` to get more accurate version names.
    *   **Returns**: The protocol name.

*   **`getVersionIntroducedIn()`**: Returns the user-friendly name of the version this protocol was introduced in.
    *   **Returns**: The version name.

*   **`getMostRecentSupportedVersion()`**: Returns the user-friendly name of the last version this protocol is valid for.
    *   **Returns**: The version name.

*   **`getVersionsSupportedBy()`**: Returns all versions this protocol is valid for.
    *   **Returns**: A list of strings representing the version names.

*   **`isSupported()`**: Returns whether this `ProtocolVersion` is supported.
    *   **Returns**: `true` if the protocol is supported, `false` otherwise.

*   **`isSupported(int protocol)`**: Returns whether the protocol is supported.
    *   **Parameters**:
        *   `protocol` (int) - The protocol as an int.
    *   **Returns**: `true` if the protocol is supported, `false` otherwise.

*   **`isSupported(ProtocolVersion version)`**: Returns whether the `ProtocolVersion` is supported.
    *   **Parameters**:
        *   `version` (ProtocolVersion) - The protocol version.
    *   **Returns**: `true` if the protocol is supported, `false` otherwise.

*   **`getProtocolVersion(int protocol)`**: Gets the `ProtocolVersion` for the given protocol.
    *   **Parameters**:
        *   `protocol` (int) - The protocol as an int.
    *   **Returns**: The `ProtocolVersion` corresponding to the given protocol number.

*   **`isUnknown()`**: Returns whether this `ProtocolVersion` is unknown to the proxy.
    *   **Returns**: `true` if the protocol is unknown, `false` otherwise.

*   **`isLegacy()`**: Returns whether this `ProtocolVersion` is a legacy protocol.
    *   **Returns**: `true` if the protocol is legacy, `false` otherwise.

*   **`toString()`**: Overrides the default `toString` method inherited from `Enum`.
    *   **Returns**: A string representation of the `ProtocolVersion` enum constant.
```

### EventManager API Endpoints

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/EventManager

This section details the methods available in the EventManager API for handling events.

```APIDOC
## POST /event/register (listener)

### Description
Requests that the specified `listener` listen for events and associate it with the `plugin`.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/event/register

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **plugin** (Object) - Required - The plugin to associate with the listener.
- **listener** (Object) - Required - The listener to register.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "plugin": "plugin_identifier",
  "listener": "listener_object"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **message** (string) - Indicates successful registration.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "message": "Listener registered successfully."
}
```
```

```APIDOC
## POST /event/register (handler)

### Description
Requests that the specified `handler` listen for events and associate it with the `plugin`. This method supports specifying the event class and registration order.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/event/register

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **plugin** (Object) - Required - The plugin to associate with the handler.
- **eventClass** (Class) - Required - The class for the event handler to register.
- **postOrder** (short or PostOrder) - Optional - The order in which events should be posted to the handler. Higher values indicate higher priority.
- **handler** (EventHandler) - Required - The handler to register.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "plugin": "plugin_identifier",
  "eventClass": "com.example.MyEvent",
  "postOrder": 10,
  "handler": "handler_object"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **message** (string) - Indicates successful registration.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "message": "Handler registered successfully."
}
```
```

```APIDOC
## POST /event/fire

### Description
Fires the specified event to the event bus asynchronously. This method returns a `CompletableFuture` that represents the posted event.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/event/fire

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **event** (Object) - Required - The event to fire.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "event": { "data": "some event data" }
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **status** (string) - Indicates the status of the asynchronous event firing.
- **eventId** (string) - A unique identifier for the fired event.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "status": "pending",
  "eventId": "unique_event_id_123"
}
```
```

```APIDOC
## POST /event/fireAndForget

### Description
Posts the specified event to the event bus and discards the result. This is a fire-and-forget operation.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/event/fireAndForget

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **event** (Object) - Required - The event to fire.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "event": { "message": "important notification" }
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **message** (string) - Indicates that the event was posted.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "message": "Event posted and result discarded."
}
```
```

```APIDOC
## DELETE /event/unregisterListener

### Description
Unregisters a specific listener for a specific plugin.

### Method
DELETE

### Endpoint
/event/unregisterListener

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **plugin** (Object) - Required - The plugin associated with the listener.
- **listener** (Object) - Required - The listener to deregister.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "plugin": "plugin_identifier",
  "listener": "listener_object"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **message** (string) - Indicates successful unregistration.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "message": "Listener unregistered successfully."
}
```
```

```APIDOC
## DELETE /event/unregister

### Description
Unregisters a specific event handler for a specific plugin.

### Method
DELETE

### Endpoint
/event/unregister

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **plugin** (Object) - Required - The plugin to associate with the handler.
- **handler** (EventHandler) - Required - The handler to unregister.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "plugin": "plugin_identifier",
  "handler": "handler_object"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **message** (string) - Indicates successful unregistration.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "message": "Handler unregistered successfully."
}
```
```

```APIDOC
## DELETE /event/unregisterListeners

### Description
Unregisters all listeners for the specified `plugin`.

### Method
DELETE

### Endpoint
/event/unregisterListeners

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **plugin** (Object) - Required - The plugin to deregister listeners for.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "plugin": "plugin_identifier"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **message** (string) - Indicates successful unregistration of all listeners for the plugin.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "message": "All listeners for the plugin unregistered successfully."
}
```
```

### SkinParts Methods

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/player/SkinParts

Provides methods to check for the presence of various skin parts.

```APIDOC
## SkinParts Methods

### Description
These methods allow checking for the presence of specific player skin parts.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
N/A

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
None

#### Query Parameters
None

#### Request Body
None

### Request Example
```json
{
  "SkinPartsInstance": "SkinParts"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **hasCape** (boolean) - True if the player has a cape, false otherwise.
- **hasHat** (boolean) - True if the player has a hat, false otherwise.
- **hasJacket** (boolean) - True if the player has a jacket, false otherwise.
- **hasLeftPants** (boolean) - True if the player has left pants, false otherwise.
- **hasRightPants** (boolean) - True if the player has right pants, false otherwise.
- **hasLeftSleeve** (boolean) - True if the player has a left sleeve, false otherwise.
- **hasRightSleeve** (boolean) - True if the player has a right sleeve, false otherwise.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "hasCape": true,
  "hasHat": false,
  "hasJacket": true,
  "hasLeftPants": true,
  "hasRightPants": false,
  "hasLeftSleeve": false,
  "hasRightSleeve": true
}
```
```

### Server Management API

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/util/class-use/Favicon

This section covers the API endpoints for managing server information and constructing server ping responses, including the use of favicons.

```APIDOC
## GET /api/servers/{server_id}

### Description
Retrieves information about a specific server, potentially including its favicon.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
/api/servers/{server_id}

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **server_id** (string) - Required - The unique identifier of the server.

### Request Example
None

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **favicon** (Optional<Favicon>) - The favicon associated with the server.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "server_id": "example_server",
  "favicon": "optional_server_favicon_data"
}
```

## POST /api/servers/ping

### Description
Constructs a server ping response, allowing for the inclusion of a favicon.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/api/servers/ping

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **version** (ServerPing.Version) - Required - The version of the server ping.
- **players** (Optional<ServerPing.Players>) - Optional - Player information.
- **description** (Component) - Required - The description of the server.
- **favicon** (Optional<Favicon>) - Optional - The favicon to include in the response.
- **modinfo** (Optional<ModInfo>) - Optional - Mod information for the server.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "version": "1.19",
  "players": {"online": 100, "max": 200},
  "description": "Welcome to our server!",
  "favicon": "base64_encoded_favicon_data"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **version** (ServerPing.Version) - The version of the server ping.
- **players** (Optional<ServerPing.Players>) - Player information.
- **description** (Component) - The description of the server.
- **favicon** (Optional<Favicon>) - The favicon included in the ping response.
- **modinfo** (Optional<ModInfo>) - Mod information.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "version": "1.19",
  "players": {"online": 100, "max": 200},
  "description": "Welcome to our server!",
  "favicon": "base64_encoded_favicon_data",
  "modinfo": {"type": "FORGE", "modList": ["example_mod"]}
}
```
```

--------------------------------

### BrigadierCommand

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/index-all

Provides static methods for creating Brigadier command argument builders.

```APIDOC
## BrigadierCommand

### Description
This class provides utility methods for building commands compatible with Brigadier, a command suggestion framework.

### Classes

#### `BrigadierCommand`

- **Description**: Represents a command compatible with the Brigadier command framework.

### Static Methods

#### `BrigadierCommand.requiredArgumentBuilder(String, ArgumentType<T>)`

- **Description**: Creates a new `RequiredArgumentBuilder` with the specified name and argument type.
- **Parameters**:
  - `name` (String) - Required - The name of the argument.
  - `type` (ArgumentType<T>) - Required - The type of the argument.
```

### Command Registration

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/index-all

APIs for registering commands with the Velocity proxy, including deprecated methods and recommendations for current usage.

```APIDOC
## Register Commands

### Description
This section details the methods available for registering commands within the Velocity proxy. It includes both deprecated methods and the recommended approach using `CommandMeta`.

### Methods

#### `CommandManager.register(BrigadierCommand)`

- **Description**: Registers a Brigadier-compatible command.
- **Deprecated**: Use `CommandManager.register(CommandMeta, Command)` instead.

#### `CommandManager.register(CommandMeta, Command)`

- **Description**: Registers the specified command with the given metadata.
- **Parameters**:
  - `meta` (CommandMeta) - Required - Metadata for the command.
  - `command` (Command) - Required - The command to register.

#### `CommandManager.register(String, Command, String...)`

- **Description**: Registers a command with a specific permission and aliases.
- **Deprecated**: Use `CommandManager.register(CommandMeta, Command)` instead.
- **Parameters**:
  - `permission` (String) - Required - The permission node required to execute the command.
  - `command` (Command) - Required - The command implementation.
  - `aliases` (String...) - Optional - Additional aliases for the command.
```

### CookieRequestEvent Methods

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/player/CookieRequestEvent

Details on the methods available for the CookieRequestEvent.

```APIDOC
## CookieRequestEvent Methods

### getResult

Returns the result associated with this event.

- **Method**: `getResult()`
- **Returns**: `CookieRequestEvent.ForwardResult`

### setResult

Sets the result of this event. The result must be non-null.

- **Method**: `setResult(CookieRequestEvent.ForwardResult result)`
- **Parameters**:
  - **result** (CookieRequestEvent.ForwardResult) - Required - The new result.

### getPlayer

Returns the player associated with this event.

- **Method**: `getPlayer()`
- **Returns**: `Player`

### getOriginalKey

Returns the original key of the cookie.

- **Method**: `getOriginalKey()`
- **Returns**: `Key`

### toString

Returns a string representation of the event.

- **Method**: `toString()`
- **Overrides**: `toString` in class `Object`
- **Returns**: `String`
```

### CommandSource in com.velocitypowered.api.proxy

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/command/class-use/CommandSource

This section explains the subinterfaces of CommandSource within the Velocity proxy API, representing different types of command executors like the console and players.

```APIDOC
## CommandSource in com.velocitypowered.api.proxy

### Description
This section outlines the different types of `CommandSource` interfaces found within the `com.velocitypowered.api.proxy` package. These interfaces specify the nature of the entity executing a command.

### Subinterfaces of CommandSource
*   **`ConsoleCommandSource`**: Represents the proxy console as the executor of a command.
*   **`Player`**: Represents a player connected to the proxy as the executor of a command.
```

### Create Minecraft Channel Identifier

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/messages/MinecraftChannelIdentifier

Demonstrates how to create a Minecraft channel identifier. This includes creating identifiers with a default namespace (minecraft) or a custom namespace. These methods are essential for inter-plugin communication and packet handling in Minecraft.

```java
import com.velocitypowered.api.proxy.messages.MinecraftChannelIdentifier;

// Create an identifier in the default namespace ('minecraft')
MinecraftChannelIdentifier defaultIdentifier = MinecraftChannelIdentifier.forDefaultNamespace("myplugin:channel");

// Create an identifier with a custom namespace
MinecraftChannelIdentifier customIdentifier = MinecraftChannelIdentifier.create("myplugin", "channel");
```

### Command Execution

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/command/CommandManager

Methods for asynchronously executing commands with or without firing command events.

```APIDOC
## POST /commands/execute/async

### Description
Attempts to asynchronously execute a command from the given command line. This method fires a `CommandExecuteEvent`.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/commands/execute/async

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **source** (CommandSource) - Required - The source of the command.
- **cmdLine** (String) - Required - The command line to execute.

### Request Example
{
  "source": "<command_source_object>",
  "cmdLine": "/help"
}

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **success** (boolean) - True if the command was executed successfully, false otherwise.

#### Response Example
{
  "success": true
}

---

## POST /commands/execute/immediate/async

### Description
Attempts to asynchronously execute a command from the given command line without firing a `CommandExecuteEvent`. This is useful for internal command execution where event firing is not desired.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/commands/execute/immediate/async

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **source** (CommandSource) - Required - The source of the command.
- **cmdLine** (String) - Required - The command line to execute.

### Request Example
{
  "source": "<command_source_object>",
  "cmdLine": "/internal_command"
}

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **success** (boolean) - True if the command was executed successfully, false otherwise.

#### Response Example
{
  "success": true
}
```

### Check Command Permission - Java

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/command/InvocableCommand

Tests if the source has the necessary permission to perform a specified invocation. If permission is denied, handling is forwarded to the player's current server. This is a default method.

```java
default boolean hasPermission(I invocation)
```

### Server Connection Events

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/index-all

Events related to player connections to backend servers, including pre-connection checks and post-connection confirmations.

```APIDOC
## Server Connection Events

### Description
These events allow for interception and modification of player connections to backend servers. `ServerPreConnectEvent` is fired before a connection is established, while `ServerPostConnectEvent` is fired after a successful connection.

### `ServerPostConnectEvent`

- **Class**: `com.velocitypowered.api.event.player.ServerPostConnectEvent`
- **Description**: Fired after a player has successfully connected to a backend server.
- **Constructor**: `ServerPostConnectEvent(Player player, RegisteredServer server)`
  - **Player**: `Player` - The player who connected.
  - **Server**: `RegisteredServer` - The server the player connected to.

### `ServerPreConnectEvent`

- **Class**: `com.velocitypowered.api.event.player.ServerPreConnectEvent`
- **Description**: This event is fired before a player connects to a backend server, allowing for modifications to the target server.
- **Constructors**:
  - `ServerPreConnectEvent(Player player, RegisteredServer targetServer)`
    - **Player**: `Player` - The player attempting to connect.
    - **Target Server**: `RegisteredServer` - The server the player is attempting to connect to.
  - `ServerPreConnectEvent(Player player, RegisteredServer originalServer, RegisteredServer targetServer)`
    - **Player**: `Player` - The player attempting to connect.
    - **Original Server**: `RegisteredServer` - The server the player was previously connected to (if any).
    - **Target Server**: `RegisteredServer` - The server the player is attempting to connect to.

- **Result Class**: `com.velocitypowered.api.event.player.ServerPreConnectEvent.ServerResult`
  Represents the result of the `ServerPreConnectEvent`, potentially containing a redirect server.
```

### Server Links API

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/Player

Sends a list of custom links to the player for display in their client's pause menu. Note that backend server packets may override these links. Requires Minecraft version 1.21 or later.

```APIDOC
## POST /setServerLinks

### Description
Send the player a list of custom links to display in their client's pause menu. Note that later packets sent by the backend server may override links sent by the proxy.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
`/setServerLinks`

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **links** (List<ServerLink>) - Required - An ordered list of `ServerLink`s to send to the player.

### Throws
- `IllegalArgumentException` - if the player is from a version lower than 1.21.
```

### CommandManager Interface - Java

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/command/CommandManager

The CommandManager interface provides methods for managing commands within the server. It handles registration, unregistration, asynchronous execution, and suggestion retrieval for commands.

```java
public interface CommandManager {
    CompletableFuture<Boolean> executeAsync(CommandSource source, String cmdLine);
    CompletableFuture<Boolean> executeImmediatelyAsync(CommandSource source, String cmdLine);
    Collection<String> getAliases();
    @Nullable CommandMeta getCommandMeta(String alias);
    boolean hasCommand(String alias);
    boolean hasCommand(String alias, CommandSource source);
    CommandMeta.Builder metaBuilder(BrigadierCommand command);
    CommandMeta.Builder metaBuilder(String alias);
    CompletableFuture<Suggestions> offerBrigadierSuggestions(CommandSource source, String cmdLine);
    CompletableFuture<List<String>> offerSuggestions(CommandSource source, String cmdLine);
    @Deprecated
    void register(BrigadierCommand command);
    void register(CommandMeta meta, Command command);
    @Deprecated
    default void register(String alias, Command command, String... otherAliases) {
        // implementation details omitted
    }
    void unregister(CommandMeta meta);
    void unregister(String alias);
}
```

### Game Profile Constructor (Java)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/index-all

Constructs a `GameProfile` object, representing a Mojang game profile. This can be done using either a username or a UUID.

```java
import com.velocitypowered.api.util.GameProfile;
import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.Collections;

// Using username
GameProfile profileByUsername = new GameProfile("PlayerName", "PlayerUUID", Collections.emptyList());

// Using UUID
UUID playerId = UUID.randomUUID();
GameProfile profileByUuid = new GameProfile(playerId, "PlayerName", Collections.emptyList());
```

### Player Interface Methods in Velocity

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/Player

This interface defines methods for interacting with a player connected to the Velocity proxy. It covers functionalities such as managing chat completions, handling resource packs, disconnecting players, retrieving player information like UUID and username, and managing server connections.

```java
public interface Player extends CommandSource, InboundConnection, ChannelMessageSource, ChannelMessageSink, Identified, HoverEventSource<HoverEvent.ShowEntity>, Keyed, KeyIdentifiable, Sound.Emitter {
    void addCustomChatCompletions(@NotNull Collection<String> completions);
    default @NotNull HoverEvent<HoverEvent.ShowEntity> asHoverEvent(@NotNull UnaryOperator<HoverEvent.ShowEntity> op);
    default void clearHeaderAndFooter();
    void clearPlayerListHeaderAndFooter();
    default void closeDialog();
    ConnectionRequestBuilder createConnectionRequest(RegisteredServer server);
    void disconnect(Component reason);
    @Nullable ResourcePackInfo getAppliedResourcePack();
    @NotNull Collection<ResourcePackInfo> getAppliedResourcePacks();
    @Nullable String getClientBrand();
    Optional<ServerConnection> getCurrentServer();
    @Nullable Locale getEffectiveLocale();
    GameProfile getGameProfile();
    List<GameProfile.Property> getGameProfileProperties();
    Optional<ModInfo> getModInfo();
    @Nullable ResourcePackInfo getPendingResourcePack();
    @NotNull Collection<ResourcePackInfo> getPendingResourcePacks();
    long getPing();
    Component getPlayerListFooter();
    Component getPlayerListHeader();
    PlayerSettings getPlayerSettings();
    TabList getTabList();
    UUID getUniqueId();
    String getUsername();
    boolean hasSentPlayerSettings();
    boolean isOnlineMode();
    default @NotNull Key key();
    default void openBook(@NotNull Book book);
    default void playSound(@NotNull Sound sound);
    default void playSound(@NotNull Sound sound, double x, double y, double z);
    default void playSound(@NotNull Sound sound, Sound.Emitter emitter);
    void removeCustomChatCompletions(@NotNull Collection<String> completions);
    void requestCookie(Key key);
    boolean sendPluginMessage(@NotNull ChannelIdentifier identifier, byte @NotNull [] data);
    boolean sendPluginMessage(@NotNull ChannelIdentifier identifier, @NotNull PluginMessageEncoder dataEncoder);
    void sendResourcePack(String url);
    void sendResourcePack(String url, byte[] hash);
    void sendResourcePackOffer(ResourcePackInfo packInfo);
    void setCustomChatCompletions(@NotNull Collection<String> completions);
    void setEffectiveLocale(Locale locale);
    void setGameProfileProperties(List<GameProfile.Property> properties);
    void setServerLinks(@NotNull List<ServerLink> links);
}
```

### Java PermissionProvider Interface for PermissionFunction Creation

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/permission/PermissionProvider

The PermissionProvider is a functional interface designed to provide PermissionFunction instances for PermissionSubject objects. It has a single abstract method, createFunction, which takes a PermissionSubject as input and returns a corresponding PermissionFunction. This interface is suitable for lambda expressions or method references.

```java
@FunctionalInterface public interface PermissionProvider
Provides `PermissionFunction`s for `PermissionSubject`s.

  PermissionFunction createFunction(PermissionSubject subject)
Creates a `PermissionFunction` for the subject.

Parameters:
    `subject` - the subject

Returns:
    the function
```

    // Nested interfaces and methods are defined here

### Registering Event Handlers

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/class-use/EventHandler

Methods for registering event handlers with the EventManager, allowing plugins to listen for specific events.

```APIDOC
## POST /event/register

### Description
Registers an event handler for a specific event class with an optional post order.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
`/event/register`

### Parameters
#### Query Parameters
- **plugin** (Object) - Required - The plugin instance.
- **eventClass** (Class) - Required - The class of the event to listen for.
- **postOrder** (short) - Optional - The order in which the event should be processed. Defaults to a standard order if not provided.
- **handler** (EventHandler) - Required - The event handler implementation.

#### Request Body
This endpoint does not typically use a request body. Parameters are passed as query parameters or method arguments.

### Request Example
```java
EventManager.register(myPlugin, MyEvent.class, (short) 100, myEventHandler);
EventManager.register(myPlugin, MyEvent.class, myEventHandler);
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
Indicates successful registration of the event handler.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "message": "Event handler registered successfully."
}
```
```

```APIDOC
## POST /event/register (Deprecated)

### Description
Deprecated method for registering an event handler. Use `EventManager.register(Object, Class, short, EventHandler)` instead.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
`/event/register`

### Parameters
#### Query Parameters
- **plugin** (Object) - Required - The plugin instance.
- **eventClass** (Class) - Required - The class of the event to listen for.
- **postOrder** (PostOrder) - Required - The post order for the event.
- **handler** (EventHandler) - Required - The event handler implementation.

#### Request Body
This endpoint does not typically use a request body. Parameters are passed as query parameters or method arguments.

### Request Example
```java
EventManager.register(myPlugin, MyEvent.class, PostOrder.NORMAL, myEventHandler);
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
Indicates successful registration of the event handler.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "message": "Event handler registered successfully."
}
```
```

### ServerPreConnectEvent API

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/player/ServerPreConnectEvent

This event is fired before the player connects to a server. Velocity will wait on this event to finish firing before initiating the connection.

```APIDOC
## ServerPreConnectEvent

### Description
This event is fired before the player connects to a server. Velocity will wait on this event to finish firing before initiating the connection.

### Method
This is an event class, typically handled by event listeners.

### Endpoint
N/A (Event Class)

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
N/A

#### Query Parameters
N/A

#### Request Body
N/A

### Request Example
```java
// This is an event, not typically instantiated directly in user code for requests.
// Example of creating the event:
ServerPreConnectEvent event = new ServerPreConnectEvent(player, originalServer);
```

### Response
#### Success Response (N/A - Event Handling)
N/A

#### Response Example
N/A

```
Creates the ServerPreConnectEvent.

**Parameters:**
- `player` (Player) - the player who is connecting to a server
- `originalServer` (RegisteredServer) - the server the player was trying to connect to

public ServerPreConnectEvent(Player player, RegisteredServer originalServer, @Nullable RegisteredServer previousServer)
```
Creates the ServerPreConnectEvent.

**Parameters:**
- `player` (Player) - the player who is connecting to a server
- `originalServer` (RegisteredServer) - the server the player was trying to connect to
- `previousServer` (@Nullable RegisteredServer) - the server the player is connected to

## Methods

### getPlayer
```java
public Player getPlayer()
```
Returns the player connecting to the server.

**Returns:**
- `Player` - the player connecting to the server

### getResult
```java
public ServerPreConnectEvent.ServerResult getResult()
```
Returns the result associated with this event.

**Returns:**
- `ServerPreConnectEvent.ServerResult` - the result of this event

### setResult
```java
public void setResult(ServerPreConnectEvent.ServerResult result)
```
Sets the result of this event. The result must be non-null.

**Parameters:**
- `result` (ServerPreConnectEvent.ServerResult) - the new result

### getOriginalServer
```java
public RegisteredServer getOriginalServer()
```
Returns the server that the player originally tried to connect to. To get the server the player will connect to, see the `ServerPreConnectEvent.ServerResult` of this event. To get the server the player is currently on when this event is fired, use `getPreviousServer()`.

**Returns:**
- `RegisteredServer` - the server that the player originally tried to connect to

### getPreviousServer
```java
public @Nullable RegisteredServer getPreviousServer()
```
Returns the server that the player is currently connected to. Prefer this method over using `Player.getCurrentServer()` as the current server might get reset after server kicks to prevent connection issues. This is `null` if they were not connected to another server beforehand (for instance, if the player has just joined the proxy).

**Returns:**
- `@Nullable RegisteredServer` - the server the player is currently connected to.

### toString
```java
public String toString()
```
Overrides: `toString` in class `Object`
```

    /**
     * Returns the timed identified key of the object context. This is only available if the client is running Minecraft 1.19 or newer.
     *
     * @return the key or null if not available
     */
    @Nullable
    IdentifiedKey getIdentifiedKey();
}

### EventTask API

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/EventTask

This section details the methods available for creating and managing EventTasks. EventTasks allow for asynchronous execution and suspension of event handling.

```APIDOC
## EventTask Interface

### Description
Represents a task that can be returned by an `EventHandler` which allows event handling to be suspended and resumed at a later time, and executing event handlers completely or partially asynchronously.

### Methods

#### `static EventTask async(Runnable task)`

##### Description
Creates a basic async `EventTask` from the given `Runnable`. The task is guaranteed to be executed asynchronously (`requiresAsync()` always returns `true`).

##### Method
`static`

##### Endpoint
N/A (Interface method)

##### Parameters
* **task** (Runnable) - Required - The task to execute asynchronously.

##### Response
* **EventTask** - The created asynchronous event task.

#### `static EventTask withContinuation(Consumer<Continuation> task)`

##### Description
Creates a continuation based `EventTask` from the given `Consumer`. The task isn't guaranteed to be executed asynchronously (`requiresAsync()` always returns `false`).

##### Method
`static`

##### Endpoint
N/A (Interface method)

##### Parameters
* **task** (Consumer<Continuation>) - Required - The task to execute using a continuation.

##### Response
* **EventTask** - The created event task.

#### `static EventTask resumeWhenComplete(CompletableFuture<?> future)`

##### Description
Creates a continuation based `EventTask` for the given `CompletableFuture`. The continuation is resumed upon completion of the given `future`, whether it is completed successfully or not.

##### Method
`static`

##### Endpoint
N/A (Interface method)

##### Parameters
* **future** (CompletableFuture<?>) - Required - The task to wait for completion.

##### Response
* **EventTask** - The created event task.

#### `void execute(Continuation continuation)`

##### Description
Runs this event task with the given `Continuation`. The continuation must be notified when the task is completed, either with `Continuation.resume()` if the task was successful or `Continuation.resumeWithException(Throwable)` if an exception occurred. The `Continuation` doesn't need to be notified during the execution of this method; this can happen at a later point in time and from another thread. The `Continuation` may only be resumed once, or an `IllegalStateException` will be thrown.

##### Method
Instance method

##### Endpoint
N/A (Interface method)

##### Parameters
* **continuation** (Continuation) - Required - The continuation to be managed by the event task.

##### Response
None.

#### `boolean requiresAsync()`

##### Description
Checks whether this `EventTask` is required to be called asynchronously. If this method returns `true`, the event task is guaranteed to be executed asynchronously from the current thread. Otherwise, the event task may be executed on the current thread or asynchronously.

##### Method
Instance method

##### Endpoint
N/A (Interface method)

##### Parameters
None.

##### Response
* **boolean** - `true` if the task requires asynchronous execution, `false` otherwise.
```

--------------------------------

### Send Plugin Message with Encoder - Java

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/messages/ChannelMessageSink

Sends a plugin message to a target using a channel identifier and a PluginMessageEncoder. This method allows for structured data encoding before sending and returns a boolean indicating success. It is an experimental feature within the ChannelMessageSink interface.

```java
@Experimental boolean sendPluginMessage(@NotNull ChannelIdentifier identifier, @NotNull PluginMessageEncoder dataEncoder)
```

```java
final ChannelMessageSink target;
final ChannelIdentifier identifier;
final boolean result = target.sendPluginMessage(identifier, (output) -> {
  output.writeUTF("some input");
  output.writeInt(1);
});

```

### Create Minecraft Channel Identifier from String or Key

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/messages/MinecraftChannelIdentifier

Shows how to create a Minecraft channel identifier from existing string representations or Velocity's Key objects. This is useful when receiving identifiers or when working with existing key-based systems.

```java
import com.velocitypowered.api.proxy.messages.MinecraftChannelIdentifier;
import com.velocitypowered.api.util.Key;

// Create from a string identifier
MinecraftChannelIdentifier fromString = MinecraftChannelIdentifier.from("myplugin:channel");

// Create from a Key object (assuming Key has been created elsewhere)
// Key myKey = Key.create("myplugin", "channel");
// MinecraftChannelIdentifier fromKey = MinecraftChannelIdentifier.from(myKey);
```

### CommandResult.SYNTAX_ERROR

Signifies that the command input contained syntax errors.

```APIDOC
### SYNTAX_ERROR

public static final CommandResult SYNTAX_ERROR

The provided command input contained syntax errors.
```

--------------------------------

### ServerResourcePackSendEvent

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/player/ServerResourcePackSendEvent

Represents an event fired when a downstream server attempts to send a player a ResourcePack packet. The proxy waits for this event to complete before forwarding the pack. If denied, a DENIED status is sent retroactively.

```APIDOC
## ServerResourcePackSendEvent API

### Description
This event is fired when the downstream server tries to send a player a ResourcePack packet. The proxy will wait on this event to finish before forwarding the resource pack to the user. If this event is denied, it will retroactively send a DENIED status to the downstream server in response. If the downstream server has it set to "forced" it will forcefully disconnect the user.

### Constructor
#### ServerResourcePackSendEvent(ResourcePackInfo receivedResourcePack, ServerConnection serverConnection)
Constructs a new ServerResourcePackSendEvent.

- **receivedResourcePack** (ResourcePackInfo) - Required - The resource pack the server sent.
- **serverConnection** (ServerConnection) - Required - The connection this occurred on.

### Methods
#### getServerConnection()
- **Returns**: ServerConnection - The server connection associated with this event.

#### getReceivedResourcePack()
- **Returns**: ResourcePackInfo - The resource pack that was received from the server.

#### getProvidedResourcePack()
- **Returns**: ResourcePackInfo - The resource pack that will be provided to the player.

#### setProvidedResourcePack(ResourcePackInfo providedResourcePack)
- **Parameters**:
  - **providedResourcePack** (ResourcePackInfo) - Required - The resource pack to provide to the player.

#### getResult()
- **Returns**: ResultedEvent.GenericResult - The result associated with this event.

#### setResult(ResultedEvent.GenericResult result)
- **Description**: Sets the result of this event. The result must be non-null.
- **Parameters**:
  - **result** (ResultedEvent.GenericResult) - Required - The new result for the event.
```

--------------------------------

### ProtocolState Enum Constants

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/network/ProtocolState

Details of the enum constants representing specific connection states: HANDSHAKE, STATUS, LOGIN, CONFIGURATION, and PLAY, with explanations of their purpose and transitions.

```java
public static final ProtocolState HANDSHAKE
Initial connection State.
This status can be caused by a `HandshakeIntent.STATUS`, `HandshakeIntent.LOGIN` or `HandshakeIntent.TRANSFER` intent.
If the intent is LOGIN or TRANSFER, the next state will be `LOGIN`, otherwise, it will go to the `STATUS` state.

public static final ProtocolState STATUS
Ping State of a connection.
Connections with the `HandshakeIntent.STATUS` intent will pass through this state and be disconnected after it requests the ping from the server and the server responds with the respective ping.

public static final ProtocolState LOGIN
Authentication State of a connection.
At this moment the player is authenticating with the authentication servers.

public static final ProtocolState CONFIGURATION
Configuration State of a connection.
At this point the player allows the server to send information such as resource packs and plugin messages, at the same time the player will send his client brand and the respective plugin messages if it is a modded client.

Since Minecraft:
    1.20.2

public static final ProtocolState PLAY
Game State of a connection.
In this state is where the whole game runs, the server is able to change the player's state to `CONFIGURATION` as needed in versions 1.20.2 and higher.
```

### DisconnectEvent.LoginStatus Enum

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/connection/DisconnectEvent

This section provides documentation for the DisconnectEvent.LoginStatus enum, including its purpose, constants, and available methods.

```APIDOC
## Enum: DisconnectEvent.LoginStatus

### Description
The status of the connection when the player disconnected.

### Enum Constants
*   **`CANCELLED_BY_PROXY`**: The disconnection was initiated by the proxy.
*   **`CANCELLED_BY_USER`**: The disconnection was initiated by the user.
*   **`CANCELLED_BY_USER_BEFORE_COMPLETE`**: The user cancelled the connection before it was fully established.
*   **`CONFLICTING_LOGIN`**: A conflicting login occurred, leading to disconnection.
*   **`PRE_SERVER_JOIN`**: The disconnection happened before the player joined a server.
*   **`SUCCESSFUL_LOGIN`**: The login was successful before disconnection.

### Methods
#### `static DisconnectEvent.LoginStatus[] values()`
Returns an array containing the constants of this enum class, in the order they are declared.

*   **Returns**: An array containing the constants of this enum class.

#### `static DisconnectEvent.LoginStatus valueOf(String name)`
Returns the enum constant of this class with the specified name. The string must match *exactly* an identifier used to declare an enum constant in this class. Extraneous whitespace characters are not permitted.

*   **Parameters**:
    *   `name` (String) - The name of the enum constant to be returned.
*   **Returns**: The enum constant with the specified name.
*   **Throws**:
    *   `IllegalArgumentException`: If this enum class has no constant with the specified name.
    *   `NullPointerException`: If the argument is null.
```

### CommandExecuteEvent

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/command/CommandExecuteEvent

This event is fired when someone executes a command. Velocity will wait for this event to finish firing before trying to handle the command and/or forwarding it to the server.

```APIDOC
## CommandExecuteEvent

### Description
This event is fired when someone executes a command. Velocity will wait for this event to finish firing before trying to handle the command and/or forwarding it to the server.

### Method
Event

### Endpoint
N/A

### Parameters
#### Constructor Parameters
- **commandSource** (CommandSource) - Required - The source executing the command.
- **command** (String) - Required - The command being executed without the first slash.
- **invocationInfo** (CommandExecuteEvent.InvocationInfo) - Optional - The invocation info of this command.

### Methods
#### `getCommandSource()`
Gets the source responsible for the execution of this command.

Returns:
- (CommandSource) the source executing the command

#### `getCommand()`
Gets the original command being executed without the first slash.

Returns:
- (String) the original command being executed

API Note:
Note that the player can provide a command that begins with spaces, but still be validly executed. For example, the command `/  velocity info`, although not valid in the chat bar, will be executed as correctly as if the player had executed `/velocity info`

#### `getInvocationInfo()`
Returns the info of the command invocation.

Returns:
- (CommandExecuteEvent.InvocationInfo) invocation info

Since: 3.4.0

#### `getResult()`
Returns the result associated with this event.

Returns:
- (CommandExecuteEvent.CommandResult) the result of this event

#### `setResult(CommandExecuteEvent.CommandResult result)`
Sets the result of this event. The result must be non-null.

Parameters:
- **result** (CommandExecuteEvent.CommandResult) - Required - the new result

#### `toString()`
Overrides the default toString method from Object.

Returns:
- (String) a string representation of the event.
```

### Java: SignedMessage Interface Definition

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/crypto/SignedMessage

Defines the SignedMessage interface, extending KeySigned. It includes methods to retrieve the signed message string, the signer's UUID, and a boolean indicating if the signature applies to a preview.

```java
public interface SignedMessage extends KeySigned {
    /**
     * Returns the signed message.
     *
     * @return the message
     */
    String getMessage();

    /**
     * Returns the signers UUID.
     *
     * @return the uuid
     */
    UUID getSignerUuid();

    /**
     * If true the signature of this message applies to a stylized component instead.
     *
     * @return signature signs preview
     */
    boolean isPreviewSigned();
}
```

### Plugin Message Sending

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/proxy/Player

APIs for sending plugin messages to player clients. These messages are intended for client-side mods and do not go to the server the player is connected to.

```APIDOC
## POST /plugin-message

### Description
Sends a plugin message to the player's client on a specified channel.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/plugin-message

### Parameters
#### Query Parameters
- **identifier** (ChannelIdentifier) - Required - The channel identifier to send the message on.

#### Request Body
- **data** (byte[]) - Required - The raw data to send as part of the plugin message.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "identifier": "my_custom_channel",
  "data": "SGVsbG8gV29ybGQ=" 
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **sentSuccessfully** (boolean) - True if the message could be sent, false otherwise.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "sentSuccessfully": true
}
```

## POST /plugin-message-encoded

### Description
Sends a plugin message to the player's client on a specified channel using an encoder function.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/plugin-message-encoded

### Parameters
#### Query Parameters
- **identifier** (ChannelIdentifier) - Required - The channel identifier to send the message on.

#### Request Body
- **dataEncoder** (PluginMessageEncoder) - Required - An object or function that encodes the data to be sent. This typically involves writing data to an output stream.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "identifier": "another_channel",
  "dataEncoder": {
    "output": [
      {"writeUTF": "some input"},
      {"writeInt": 1}
    ]
  }
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **sentSuccessfully** (boolean) - True if the message could be sent, false otherwise.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "sentSuccessfully": true
}
```
```

```APIDOC
## PUT /api/profiles/game

### Description
Updates or creates a game profile with specified details.

### Method
PUT

### Endpoint
/api/profiles/game

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **currentProfile** (object) - Optional - The existing GameProfile to modify.
  - **id** (string) - Optional - The UUID of the player.
  - **name** (string) - Optional - The username of the player.
  - **properties** (array) - Optional - A list of properties for the player.
    - **name** (string) - Required - The name of the property.
    - **value** (string) - Required - The value of the property.
    - **signature** (string) - Optional - The signature of the property.
- **newId** (string) - Optional - A new UUID to assign to the profile.
- **newName** (string) - Optional - A new username to assign to the profile.
- **newProperties** (array) - Optional - A list of new properties to assign.
  - **name** (string) - Required - The name of the property.
  - **value** (string) - Required - The value of the property.
  - **signature** (string) - Optional - The signature of the property.

### Request Example

{
  "currentProfile": {
    "id": "a1b2c3d4-e5f6-7890-1234-567890abcdef",
    "name": "ExamplePlayer"
  },
  "newName": "UpdatedPlayerName",
  "newProperties": [
    {
      "name": "textures",
      "value": "<base64 encoded texture data>",
      "signature": "<base64 encoded signature>"
    }
  ]
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **updatedProfile** (object) - The modified GameProfile.
  - **id** (string) - The UUID of the player.
  - **name** (string) - The username of the player.
  - **properties** (array) - A list of properties for the player.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "updatedProfile": {
    "id": "a1b2c3d4-e5f6-7890-1234-567890abcdef",
    "name": "UpdatedPlayerName",
    "properties": [
      {
        "name": "textures",
        "value": "<base64 encoded texture data>",
        "signature": "<base64 encoded signature>"
      }
    ]
  }
}
```
```

### PlayerResourcePackStatusEvent.Status Enum Constants

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/player/PlayerResourcePackStatusEvent

Defines the possible states for a player's resource pack status. These constants represent actions like accepting, declining, or encountering errors during download or application. This enum is part of the PaperMC Velocity API.

```java
public static enum PlayerResourcePackStatusEvent.Status extends Enum<PlayerResourcePackStatusEvent.Status>

Enum Constants
Enum Constant
Description
`ACCEPTED`
The player has accepted the resource pack and is now downloading it.
`DECLINED`
The player declined to download the resource pack.
`DISCARDED`
The resource pack was discarded.
`DOWNLOADED`
The player has downloaded the resource pack.
`FAILED_DOWNLOAD`
The player could not download the resource pack.
`FAILED_RELOAD`
The player failed to reload the resource pack.
`INVALID_URL`
The URL of the resource pack failed to load.
`SUCCESSFUL`
The resource pack was applied successfully.
```

### Scheduler TaskBuilder

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/index-all

Defines methods for configuring the repetition of scheduled tasks.

```APIDOC
## Scheduler TaskBuilder

### Description
This section details how to configure the repetition of tasks using the `Scheduler.TaskBuilder`.

### Interfaces

#### `Scheduler.TaskBuilder`

- **Description**: Builder for configuring scheduled tasks.

### Methods

#### `Scheduler.TaskBuilder.repeat(long, TimeUnit)`

- **Description**: Specifies that the task should repeat after a given time unit duration.
- **Parameters**:
  - `duration` (long) - Required - The amount of time.
  - `unit` (TimeUnit) - Required - The time unit for the duration.

#### `Scheduler.TaskBuilder.repeat(Duration)`

- **Description**: Specifies that the task should repeat after a given `Duration`.
- **Parameters**:
  - `duration` (Duration) - Required - The duration to repeat after.
```

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/player/CookieStoreEvent

Velocity will wait on this event to finish firing before discarding the cookie (if handled) or forwarding it to the client so that it can store the cookie.

### ProxyPingEvent

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/proxy/ProxyPingEvent

This event is fired when a request for server information is sent by a remote client, or when the server sends the MOTD and favicon to the client after a successful login. Velocity will wait on this event to finish firing before delivering the results to the remote client, but you are urged to handle this event as quickly as possible when handling this event due to the amount of ping packets a client can send.

```APIDOC
## ProxyPingEvent

### Description
This event is fired when a request for server information is sent by a remote client, or when the server sends the MOTD and favicon to the client after a successful login. Velocity will wait on this event to finish firing before delivering the results to the remote client, but you are urged to handle this event as quickly as possible when handling this event due to the amount of ping packets a client can send.

### Method
Event

### Endpoint
N/A

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
None

#### Query Parameters
None

#### Request Body
None

### Request Example
```json
{
  "example": "No request body"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (Event Fired)
- **connection** (InboundConnection) - The connection that has sent the ServerPing request.
- **ping** (ServerPing) - The ServerPing object to send to the connection.
- **result** (ResultedEvent.GenericResult) - Indicates whether to avoid sending a ping response to the connection.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "connection": "InboundConnection object",
  "ping": "ServerPing object",
  "result": "ResultedEvent.GenericResult enum value"
}
```

### Methods
- **getConnection()**: Obtains the connection to which the corresponding ServerPing will be sent.
- **getPing()**: Gets the ServerPing to send to the connection.
- **setPing(@NotNull ServerPing ping)**: Sets the ServerPing to send to the connection.
- **getResult()**: Gets whether to avoid sending a ping response to the connection.
- **setResult(@NotNull ResultedEvent.GenericResult result)**: Sets whether to avoid sending a ping response to the connection. This will automatically close the connection.
- **toString()**: Returns a string representation of the event.
```

### Server Resource Pack Removal Event

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/player/ServerResourcePackRemoveEvent

This event is triggered when the downstream server attempts to remove a resource pack from a player or clear all resource packs. The proxy will await the completion of this event before proceeding with the action. If the event is denied, no resource packs will be removed.

```APIDOC
## Event: ServerResourcePackRemoveEvent

### Description
This event fires when the downstream server tries to remove a resource pack from a player or clear all of them. The proxy waits for this event to complete before forwarding the action to the user. Denying this event prevents resource pack removal.

### Method
Event Triggered (Asynchronous)

### Endpoint
N/A (Event-driven)

### Parameters
#### Constructor Parameters
- **packId** (UUID) - Optional - The ID of the resource pack to remove. If null, all resource packs will be cleared.
- **serverConnection** (ServerConnection) - Required - The server connection attempting the removal.

### Request Example
N/A (Event-based, instantiated internally)

### Response
#### Event Result
- **ResultedEvent.GenericResult** - The result of the event, indicating whether the action should proceed or be cancelled.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "result": "ALLOWED" // or "DENIED"
}
```

### Methods
- **getPackId()** (UUID): Returns the ID of the resource pack, or null if all packs are to be cleared.
- **getServerConnection()** (ServerConnection): Returns the server connection initiating the removal.
- **getResult()** (ResultedEvent.GenericResult): Retrieves the current result of the event.
- **setResult(ResultedEvent.GenericResult result)**: Sets the result of the event. Must be non-null.
```

### EventHandler Interface Definition (Java)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/EventHandler

Defines the EventHandler functional interface for direct event dispatch. It can be used with lambda expressions and method references, and facilitates integration with platforms like RxJava. The interface includes methods for synchronous and asynchronous event execution.

```java
@FunctionalInterface
public interface EventHandler<E> {
    void execute(E event);

    default @Nullable EventTask executeAsync(E event) {
        // Default implementation would typically be provided elsewhere or left abstract
        return null; // Placeholder for default implementation
    }
}
```

    *   Parameters:
        *   `name` (String) - Required - The name of the enum constant to be returned.
    *   Return Type: `ConnectionRequestBuilder.Status`
    *   Throws:
        *   `IllegalArgumentException` - if this enum class has no constant with the specified name
        *   `NullPointerException` - if the argument is null

--------------------------------

### ProtocolState Constants Details

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/network/ProtocolState

Detailed descriptions for each enum constant within the ProtocolState enum.

```APIDOC
## Enum Constant Details

### HANDSHAKE
`public static final ProtocolState HANDSHAKE`
Initial connection State.

This status can be caused by a `HandshakeIntent.STATUS`, `HandshakeIntent.LOGIN` or `HandshakeIntent.TRANSFER` intent. If the intent is LOGIN or TRANSFER, the next state will be `LOGIN`, otherwise, it will go to the `STATUS` state.

### STATUS
`public static final ProtocolState STATUS`
Ping State of a connection.

Connections with the `HandshakeIntent.STATUS` intent will pass through this state and be disconnected after it requests the ping from the server and the server responds with the respective ping.

### LOGIN
`public static final ProtocolState LOGIN`
Authentication State of a connection.

At this moment the player is authenticating with the authentication servers.

### CONFIGURATION
`public static final ProtocolState CONFIGURATION`
Configuration State of a connection.

At this point the player allows the server to send information such as resource packs and plugin messages, at the same time the player will send his client brand and the respective plugin messages if it is a modded client.

Since Minecraft: 1.20.2

### PLAY
`public static final ProtocolState PLAY`
Game State of a connection.

In this state is where the whole game runs, the server is able to change the player's state to `CONFIGURATION` as needed in versions 1.20.2 and higher.
```

--------------------------------

## Enum: ProtocolState

### Description
Representation of the state of the protocol in which a connection can be present.

Since: 3.3.0

### Enum Constants

*   **CONFIGURATION**
    Configuration State of a connection.

*   **HANDSHAKE**
    Initial connection State.

*   **LOGIN**
    Authentication State of a connection.

*   **PLAY**
    Game State of a connection.

*   **STATUS**
    Ping State of a connection.

### Methods

    Returns an array containing the constants of this enum class, in the order they are declared.

*   **valueOf(String name)**
    Returns the enum constant of this class with the specified name.

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/index-all

Enum constants indicating the signed state of a command execution, differentiating between commands with and without signed arguments.

```APIDOC
## CommandExecuteEvent.SignedState

### Description
Indicates the signed state of a command execution.

### Enum Constants
- **SIGNED_WITH_ARGS**: Indicates that the command was executed from a signed source with signed message arguments. This is currently only possible by typing a command in chat with signed arguments.
- **SIGNED_WITHOUT_ARGS**: Indicates that the command was executed from an signed source with no signed message arguments. This is currently only possible by typing a command in chat.

### Endpoint
N/A (Enum constants)

### Parameters
None

### Request Example
```java
// Example of checking the signed state of a command
CommandExecuteEvent.SignedState state = event.getInvocationInfo().signedState();
if (state == CommandExecuteEvent.SignedState.SIGNED_WITH_ARGS) {
    // Handle command with signed arguments
}
```

### TabListEntry Builder gameMode (Java)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/index-all

Sets the game mode for a `TabListEntry.Builder`. This is used to customize the appearance of entries in the player list.

```java
import com.velocitypowered.api.proxy.player.TabListEntry;
import net.kyori.adventure.text.Component;

// Assuming 'builder' is an instance of TabListEntry.Builder
TabListEntry.Builder builder = TabListEntry.builder();
// The actual game mode value would be an integer representing the game mode ID.
// For example, 0 for Survival, 1 for Creative, etc.
int gameModeId = 1; 
builder.gameMode(gameModeId);
// You would then typically add other properties like displayName, UUID, etc.
// TabListEntry entry = builder.build();
```

### Verify Data Signature

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/index-all

Validates a signature against a public key using the IdentifiedKey interface.

```APIDOC
## POST /api/keys/verify-signature

### Description
Validates a signature against a public key.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/api/keys/verify-signature

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **publicKey** (string) - Required - The public key in a suitable format (e.g., PEM encoded).
- **signature** (string) - Required - The signature to verify.
- **data** (array of strings) - Required - The data that was signed, as an array of byte arrays (represented as strings).

### Request Example
```json
{
  "publicKey": "-----BEGIN PUBLIC KEY-----\n...\n-----END PUBLIC KEY-----",
  "signature": "<base64 encoded signature>",
  "data": ["<base64 encoded byte array 1>", "<base64 encoded byte array 2>"]
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **isValid** (boolean) - True if the signature is valid, false otherwise.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "isValid": true
}
```
```

### Event Result Handling

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/index-all

Interfaces and classes for handling results of events, including generic and component-based results.

```APIDOC
## Event Result Handling

### Description
This section covers the mechanisms for handling results from events, allowing for outcomes like allowing or denying an action with optional reasons.

### Interfaces

#### `ResultedEvent<R extends ResultedEvent.Result>`

- **Description**: Interface for events that have an associated result.

#### `ResultedEvent.Result`

- **Description**: Represents a result for an event.

### Classes

#### `ResultedEvent.ComponentResult`

- **Description**: Represents an "allowed/denied" result that includes a reason, typically for denial.

#### `ResultedEvent.GenericResult`

- **Description**: A simple "allowed/denied" result without an explicit reason.
```

### PluginMessageEvent

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/connection/PluginMessageEvent

Represents an event fired when a plugin message is sent to the proxy, either from a client or a server.

```APIDOC
## PluginMessageEvent

### Description
This event is fired when a plugin message is sent to the proxy, either from a client (`Player`) or a server (`ServerConnection`). Velocity will wait on this event to finish firing before discarding the sent plugin message (if handled) or forwarding it to the server.

### Method
Event

### Endpoint
N/A

### Parameters
#### Constructor Parameters
- **source** (ChannelMessageSource) - The source of the plugin message.
- **target** (ChannelMessageSink) - The destination of the plugin message.
- **identifier** (ChannelIdentifier) - The channel for this plugin message.
- **data** (byte[]) - The payload of the plugin message.

### Methods
#### `getResult()`
- **Returns**: `PluginMessageEvent.ForwardResult` - The result associated with this event.

#### `setResult(PluginMessageEvent.ForwardResult result)`
- **Parameters**:
  - **result** (PluginMessageEvent.ForwardResult) - The new result. Must be non-null.

#### `getSource()`
- **Returns**: `ChannelMessageSource` - The source of the plugin message.

#### `getTarget()`
- **Returns**: `ChannelMessageSink` - The destination of the plugin message.

#### `getIdentifier()`
- **Returns**: `ChannelIdentifier` - The channel for this plugin message.

#### `getData()`
- **Returns**: `byte[]` - The payload of the plugin message.

#### `dataAsInputStream()`
- **Returns**: `ByteArrayInputStream` - The payload of the plugin message as an InputStream.

#### `dataAsDataStream()`
- **Returns**: `ByteArrayDataInput` - The payload of the plugin message as a ByteArrayDataInput.

#### `toString()`
- **Overrides**: `toString` in class `Object`
- **Returns**: `String` - A string representation of the event.

### Nested Classes
#### `PluginMessageEvent.ForwardResult`
- **Description**: A result determining whether or not to forward this message on.

```

### PostOrder Enum Constants and Methods (Java)

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/PostOrder

This snippet shows the declaration of the PostOrder enum in Java, including its constants (FIRST, EARLY, NORMAL, LATE, LAST, CUSTOM) and the static methods `values()` and `valueOf()`. The `CUSTOM` constant is deprecated. These are used to control the order in which event listeners are invoked.

```java
public enum PostOrder extends Enum<PostOrder> {
    CUSTOM,
    EARLY,
    FIRST,
    LAST,
    LATE,
    NORMAL
}

public static PostOrder[] values()
public static PostOrder valueOf(String name)
```

### Tristate Enum Constants and Methods - Java

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/permission/Tristate

Defines the Tristate enum with its constants TRUE, FALSE, and UNDEFINED. Provides static methods to create Tristate from boolean, nullable boolean, and optional boolean values, as well as an instance method to convert Tristate to a boolean and to an Adventure TriState. Also includes standard enum methods like values() and valueOf().

```java
package com.velocitypowered.api.permission;

import java.util.Optional;

public enum Tristate implements Serializable, Comparable<Tristate>, Constable {
    TRUE,
    FALSE,
    UNDEFINED;

    public static Tristate fromBoolean(boolean val) {
        // Implementation details...
        return val ? TRUE : FALSE;
    }

    public static Tristate fromNullableBoolean(@Nullable Boolean val) {
        // Implementation details...
        if (val == null) {
            return UNDEFINED;
        }
        return val ? TRUE : FALSE;
    }

    public static Tristate fromOptionalBoolean(Optional<Boolean> val) {
        // Implementation details...
        if (val.isEmpty()) {
            return UNDEFINED;
        }
        return val.get() ? TRUE : FALSE;
    }

    public boolean asBoolean() {
        // Implementation details...
        return this == TRUE;
    }

    public TriState toAdventureTriState() {
        // Implementation details...
        return null; // Placeholder for actual implementation
    }

    public static Tristate valueOf(String name) {
        // Implementation details...
        return Enum.valueOf(Tristate.class, name);
    }

    public static Tristate[] values() {
        // Implementation details...
        return new Tristate[]{TRUE, FALSE, UNDEFINED};
    }
}
```

### Subscribe Annotation - Event Handling

Source: https://jd.papermc.io/velocity/3.4.0/com/velocitypowered/api/event/Subscribe

The @Subscribe annotation marks a method as an event listener. It provides options to control the execution order, priority, and asynchronous nature of event handlers.

```APIDOC
## @Subscribe Annotation

### Description
An annotation that indicates that this method can be used to listen for an event from the proxy. It allows configuration of event handler behavior.

### Method
Annotation

### Endpoint
N/A

### Parameters
#### Optional Element Summary
- **async** (boolean) - Optional - Whether the handler must be called asynchronously. Defaults to `true`.
- **order** (PostOrder) - Deprecated - Specifies the order using `priority()` instead.
- **priority** (short) - Optional - The priority of this event handler. Higher values are executed earlier. Defaults to `0`.

### Request Example
```java
@Subscribe(async = true, priority = 10)
public void onPlayerJoin(PlayerChooseEvent event) {
    // Handle player join event
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (N/A)
This is an annotation, not an endpoint that returns a response.

#### Response Example
N/A

### Element Details

#### async
`boolean async`
Whether the handler must be called asynchronously. By default, all event handlers are called asynchronously. For performance (for instance, if you use `EventTask.withContinuation(java.util.function.Consumer<com.velocitypowered.api.event.Continuation>)`), you can optionally specify `false`. This option will become `false` by default in a future release of Velocity.
If this is `true`, the method is guaranteed to be executed asynchronously. Otherwise, the handler may be executed on the current thread or asynchronously. **This still means you must consider thread-safety in your event listeners** as the "current thread" can and will be different each time.
Note that if any method handler targeting an event type is marked with `true`, then every handler targeting that event type will be executed asynchronously.

Returns: `Requires async`
Default: `true`

#### order
`@Deprecated PostOrder order`
Deprecated. specify the order using `priority()` instead
The order events will be posted to this listener.

Returns: `the order`
Default: `NORMAL`

#### priority
`short priority`
The priority of this event handler. Priorities are used to determine the order in which event handlers are called. The higher the priority, the earlier the event handler will be called.

Returns: `the priority`
Default: `0`
```
